STANDALONE FRONTEND RUN STEPS:
  1. Extract files into a `frontend/` folder (or copy individual files).
  2. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and set `VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:3000` (or your Render URL).
  3. Copy the 4 campus images into `src/assets/images/`:
     - src/assets/images/hero_school_campus_1790606578302.jpg
     - src/assets/images/school_library_hall_1790606612341.jpg
     - src/assets/images/school_science_lab_1790606594349.jpg
     - src/assets/images/school_sports_ground_1790606627159.jpg
  4. Run `npm install` then `npm run dev` (starts Vite on http://localhost:5173) or `npm run build`.

// === FILE_START: vite.config.ts ===
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import {defineConfig} from 'vite';

function copySrcAssetsPlugin() {
  return {
    name: 'copy-src-assets',
    closeBundle() {
      const rootDir = import.meta.dirname ?? '.';
      const srcAssets = path.resolve(rootDir, 'src', 'assets');
      const distSrcAssets = path.resolve(rootDir, 'dist', 'src', 'assets');
      if (fs.existsSync(srcAssets)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(distSrcAssets), {recursive: true});
        fs.cpSync(srcAssets, distSrcAssets, {recursive: true});
      }
    },
  };
}

export default defineConfig(() => {
  return {
    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss(), copySrcAssetsPlugin()],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(import.meta.dirname ?? '.', '.'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.
      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.
      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',
      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.
      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},
    },
  };
});
// === FILE_END: vite.config.ts ===

// === FILE_START: src/App.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { RefreshCw, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './context/AuthContext';
import { api, buildApiUrl } from './services/api';
import {
  SchoolSettings,
  ClassItem,
  SubjectItem,
  Teacher,
  Announcement,
  SchoolEvent,
  GalleryItem,
} from './types';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { NoticeBanner } from './components/NoticeBanner';
import { HeroSection } from './components/HeroSection';
import { AboutSection } from './components/AboutSection';
import { AcademicsSection } from './components/AcademicsSection';
import { AdmissionsSection } from './components/AdmissionsSection';
import { FacilitiesSection } from './components/FacilitiesSection';
import { TeachersSection } from './components/TeachersSection';
import { FeesSection } from './components/FeesSection';
import { NewsEventsSection } from './components/NewsEventsSection';
import { GallerySection } from './components/GallerySection';
import { ContactLocationSection } from './components/ContactLocationSection';
import { Footer } from './components/Footer';
import { LoginModal } from './components/LoginModal';
import { StudentPortal } from './components/StudentPortal';
import { TeacherPortal } from './components/TeacherPortal';
import { AdminDashboard } from './components/AdminDashboard';

function getScrollBehavior(): ScrollBehavior {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
    return 'auto';
  }
  return 'smooth';
}

function SchoolApp() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState<'public' | 'portal' | 'admin' | 'teacher'>('public');
  const [loginModalOpen, setLoginModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [pendingResetToken, setPendingResetToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [preselectedClass, setPreselectedClass] = useState<string>('Grade 9 - Matric');

  // School Public Data
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<SchoolSettings | null>(null);
  const [classes, setClasses] = useState<ClassItem[]>([]);
  const [subjects, setSubjects] = useState<SubjectItem[]>([]);
  const [teachers, setTeachers] = useState<Teacher[]>([]);
  const [announcements, setAnnouncements] = useState<Announcement[]>([]);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<SchoolEvent[]>([]);
  const [gallery, setGallery] = useState<GalleryItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchPublicData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setLoadError(null);
    let lastErr: any = null;

    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 4; attempt++) {
      try {
        const [sets, cls, subs, tchs, anns, evs, gals] = await Promise.all([
          api.getSettings(),
          api.getClasses(),
          api.getSubjects(),
          api.getTeachers(),
          api.getAnnouncements(),
          api.getEvents(),
          api.getGallery(),
        ]);

        if (!sets || typeof sets !== 'object' || !sets.school_name) {
          throw new Error('Invalid school settings payload received from backend.');
        }

        setSettings(sets);
        setClasses(Array.isArray(cls) ? cls : []);
        setSubjects(Array.isArray(subs) ? subs : []);
        setTeachers(Array.isArray(tchs) ? tchs : []);
        setAnnouncements(Array.isArray(anns) ? anns : []);
        setEvents(Array.isArray(evs) ? evs : []);
        setGallery(Array.isArray(gals) ? gals : []);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      } catch (err: any) {
        lastErr = err;
        const msg = String(err?.message || '');
        const isTransientNetwork =
          err instanceof TypeError || /failed to fetch|networkerror|load failed/i.test(msg);
        if (isTransientNetwork && attempt < 3) {
          await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1500));
          continue;
        }
        if (attempt === 0 && /temporarily unavailable/i.test(msg)) {
          try {
            const hRes = await fetch(buildApiUrl('/health'), { credentials: 'include' });
            const hJson = await hRes.json().catch(() => null);
            if (hJson?.connected === true || (hJson?.database?.state === 'connecting' && !hJson?.error)) {
              await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1500));
              continue;
            }
          } catch {
            // Proceed to diagnostic error handling below
          }
        }
        break;
      }
    }

    let detailedMessage =
      'Unable to load school portal data at this moment. Please check your connection and retry.';
    try {
      const healthRes = await fetch(buildApiUrl('/health'), { credentials: 'include' });
      const healthData = await healthRes.json().catch(() => null);
      if (healthData && healthData.connected === false) {
        if (healthData.error) {
          detailedMessage = `MongoDB Atlas connection is currently unreachable (${healthData.error}). Once the current server IP is allowlisted in MongoDB Atlas Network Access, the portal will connect automatically.`;
        } else {
          detailedMessage =
            'Database connection is currently unavailable or still initializing. Please verify MongoDB Atlas connectivity and retry.';
        }
      } else if (lastErr?.message && /VITE_API_BASE_URL|Invalid API response/i.test(String(lastErr.message))) {
        detailedMessage = String(lastErr.message);
      }
    } catch {
      if (lastErr?.message && /VITE_API_BASE_URL|Invalid API response/i.test(String(lastErr.message))) {
        detailedMessage = String(lastErr.message);
      }
    }

    setLoadError(detailedMessage);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchPublicData();
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      const queryToken = searchParams.get('reset_token');
      const hashMatch = window.location.hash.match(/reset_token=([a-fA-F0-9]{64})/);
      const detectedToken =
        queryToken && /^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$/.test(queryToken.trim())
          ? queryToken.trim()
          : hashMatch
          ? hashMatch[1]
          : null;
      if (detectedToken) {
        setPendingResetToken(detectedToken);
        setLoginModalOpen(true);
        searchParams.delete('reset_token');
        const remainingQuery = searchParams.toString();
        const cleanUrl = `${window.location.pathname}${remainingQuery ? `?${remainingQuery}` : ''}`;
        window.history.replaceState({}, document.title, cleanUrl);
      }
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (settings || loading || !loadError) return;
    const pollTimer = window.setInterval(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(buildApiUrl('/health'), { credentials: 'include' });
        const data = await res.json().catch(() => null);
        if (res.ok && data?.connected === true) {
          fetchPublicData();
        }
      } catch {
        // Ignore background poll errors while waiting for database connectivity
      }
    }, 10000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(pollTimer);
  }, [settings, loading, loadError]);

  const scrollToSection = (sectionId: string) => {
    setCurrentView('public');
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el = document.getElementById(sectionId);
      if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: getScrollBehavior() });
    }, 80);
  };

  const handleApplyForClass = (className: string) => {
    setPreselectedClass(className);
    scrollToSection('admissions');
  };

  const handleOpenAdmission = () => {
    scrollToSection('admissions');
  };

  const handleNavigateSection = (sectionId: string) => {
    scrollToSection(sectionId);
  };

  if (loading && !settings) {
    return (
      <div
        role="status"
        aria-live="polite"
        className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-950 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      >
        <div className="text-center space-y-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 border-2 border-emerald-500 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
          <h1 className="text-white text-lg font-bold font-display tracking-tight">
            Shawan School &amp; College
          </h1>
          <p className="text-neutral-400 text-xs">Paharpur, Dera Ismail Khan, Pakistan</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!settings) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 shadow-sm p-6 sm:p-8 max-w-md w-full text-center space-y-4">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
            <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-lg font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display">Shawan School &amp; College</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
              {loadError || 'Unable to connect to the institutional server.'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={fetchPublicData}
            className="min-h-[40px] px-5 py-2.5 bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl inline-flex items-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Retry Connection</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Render Student / Parent Portal View
  if (currentView === 'portal') {
    return <StudentPortal onBackToWebsite={() => setCurrentView('public')} />;
  }

  // Render Admin Console View
  if (currentView === 'admin') {
    return (
      <AdminDashboard
        onBackToWebsite={() => setCurrentView('public')}
        onRefreshSiteSettings={fetchPublicData}
      />
    );
  }

  // Render Teacher Portal View
  if (currentView === 'teacher') {
    return <TeacherPortal onBackToWebsite={() => setCurrentView('public')} />;
  }

  // Render Public Website
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen w-full max-w-full overflow-x-clip bg-neutral-50 flex flex-col text-neutral-900 selection:bg-emerald-900 selection:text-white">
      {/* Keyboard Accessibility Skip Link */}
      <a
        href="#main-content"
        className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:fixed focus:top-3 focus:left-3 focus:z-50 focus:px-4 focus:py-2.5 focus:bg-emerald-950 focus:text-white focus:rounded-xl focus:text-xs focus:font-semibold focus:shadow-lg"
      >
        Skip to main content
      </a>

      {/* Notice Banner */}
      <NoticeBanner
        announcements={announcements}
        onViewNotice={() => handleNavigateSection('news-events')}
      />

      {/* Top Navbar adhering to 3-zone contract */}
      <Navbar
        onOpenLogin={() => setLoginModalOpen(true)}
        onOpenPortal={() => {
          if (!user) {
            setLoginModalOpen(true);
          } else if (['super_admin', 'admin'].includes(user.role)) {
            setCurrentView('admin');
          } else if (user.role === 'teacher') {
            setCurrentView('teacher');
          } else {
            setCurrentView('portal');
          }
        }}
        onOpenAdmission={handleOpenAdmission}
        currentView={currentView}
        setCurrentView={setCurrentView}
      />

      {/* Main Website Sections */}
      <main id="main-content" className="w-full max-w-full overflow-x-clip flex-1">
        {/* 1. Hero */}
        <HeroSection
          settings={settings}
          onApplyClick={handleOpenAdmission}
          onExploreClick={() => handleNavigateSection('about')}
        />

        {/* 2. About & Leadership Message */}
        <AboutSection
          settings={settings}
          onExploreAcademics={() => handleNavigateSection('academics')}
        />

        {/* 3. Academics & Curricula */}
        <AcademicsSection
          classes={classes}
          subjects={subjects}
          onApplyForClass={handleApplyForClass}
        />

        {/* 4. Online Admissions & Tracking */}
        <AdmissionsSection
          classes={classes}
          preselectedClass={preselectedClass}
        />

        {/* 5. Campus Facilities */}
        <FacilitiesSection settings={settings} />

        {/* 6. Faculty & Mentors */}
        <TeachersSection teachers={teachers} />

        {/* 7. Fee Schedule */}
        <FeesSection classes={classes} onOpenAdmission={handleOpenAdmission} />

        {/* 8. News, Circulars & Upcoming Events */}
        <NewsEventsSection announcements={announcements} events={events} />

        {/* 9. Visual Gallery */}
        <GallerySection gallery={gallery} />

        {/* 10. Contact & Verified Google Maps Location */}
        <ContactLocationSection settings={settings} />
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <Footer
        settings={settings}
        onNavigate={handleNavigateSection}
        onOpenPortal={() => {
          if (!user) setLoginModalOpen(true);
          else if (['super_admin', 'admin'].includes(user.role)) setCurrentView('admin');
          else if (user.role === 'teacher') setCurrentView('teacher');
          else setCurrentView('portal');
        }}
      />

      {/* Authentication Modal */}
      <LoginModal
        isOpen={loginModalOpen}
        initialResetToken={pendingResetToken}
        onClearResetToken={() => setPendingResetToken(null)}
        onClose={() => {
          setLoginModalOpen(false);
          setPendingResetToken(null);
        }}
        onSuccess={role => {
          if (['super_admin', 'admin'].includes(role)) {
            setCurrentView('admin');
          } else if (role === 'teacher') {
            setCurrentView('teacher');
          } else {
            setCurrentView('portal');
          }
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <SchoolApp />
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}
// === FILE_END: src/App.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/AboutSection.tsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { Target, Compass, CheckCircle2, Quote, MapPin, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { SchoolSettings } from '../types';

interface AboutSectionProps {
  settings: SchoolSettings;
  onExploreAcademics: () => void;
}

export const AboutSection: React.FC<AboutSectionProps> = ({ settings, onExploreAcademics }) => {
  return (
    <section id="about" className="py-16 sm:py-20 bg-white border-b border-neutral-200">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        {/* Section Header */}
        <div className="max-w-3xl mb-12 sm:mb-16">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 tracking-wider uppercase mb-2">
            Institutional Identity &amp; Leadership
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-neutral-950 font-display tracking-tight text-balance mb-4">
            Committed to Educational Distinction in Paharpur
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed text-pretty">
            {settings.about_text ||
              'Established to deliver modern academic standards in Paharpur, Dera Ismail Khan, Shawan School and College integrates rigorous science curricula with moral tarbiyah and disciplined pedagogy.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Asymmetric Grid: Story + Principal's Message */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 mb-14 items-start">
          {/* Left: Academic Approach & Location Verification */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-6">
            <div className="bg-neutral-50 rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 border border-neutral-200">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display mb-3">
                Academic Approach &amp; Regional Context
              </h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed mb-4">
                Situated prominently on Rangpur Road in Paharpur, our institution serves students from across the tehsil
                seeking structured preparation for secondary board examinations (SSC) and higher secondary college (HSSC)
                in Science, Pre-Medical, Pre-Engineering, and Computer Science.
              </p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-4 border-t border-neutral-200 text-xs text-neutral-700">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                  <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-800 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <div>
                    <span className="font-semibold block text-neutral-900">Verified Location</span>
                    <span>4X48+MRH, Rangpur Road, Paharpur, D.I. Khan</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                  <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-800 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <div>
                    <span className="font-semibold block text-neutral-900">Operating Schedule</span>
                    <span>{settings.school_timings_summer || '07:45 AM - 01:30 PM'}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Mission & Vision Cards */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 shadow-2xs">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200/70 text-emerald-900 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                  <Target className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display mb-2">Our Mission</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
                  {settings.mission ||
                    'To cultivate intellectually competent, morally upright, and socially proactive leaders through rigorous academic training and STEM education.'}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 shadow-2xs">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200/70 text-emerald-900 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                  <Compass className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display mb-2">Our Vision</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
                  {settings.vision ||
                    'To stand as the leading center of academic excellence in the Dera Ismail Khan division, nurturing students who excel nationally.'}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right: Principal's Official Message */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-emerald-950 text-white rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-9 border border-emerald-900 shadow-md relative overflow-hidden">
            <div aria-hidden="true" className="absolute top-6 right-6 opacity-10 pointer-events-none">
              <Quote className="w-20 h-20 text-white" />
            </div>

            <div className="relative z-10">
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-400 tracking-wider uppercase mb-3">
                Message from Leadership
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold font-display mb-4 text-emerald-50">
                “Educating Minds, Cultivating Character”
              </h3>

              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-200 leading-relaxed mb-6 font-normal">
                {settings.principal_message ||
                  'Welcome to Shawan School and College Paharpur. Our mission is to democratize world-class scientific, intellectual, and moral instruction for the youth of Paharpur. We foster an academic environment where disciplined study, equipped laboratories, dedicated mentors, and Islamic ethical values empower each student to realize their highest academic and career aspirations.'}
              </p>

              <div className="pt-5 border-t border-emerald-900 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-white font-display">
                    {settings.principal_name || 'Office of the Principal'}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-emerald-400">Shawan School and College, Paharpur</div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onExploreAcademics}
                  className="min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-900/80 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  View Programs →
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Core Values Strip */}
        {(settings.core_values || []).length > 0 && (
          <div className="border-t border-neutral-200 pt-10">
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-5">
              Institutional Values &amp; Guiding Pillars
            </div>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              {(settings.core_values || []).map((val, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={idx}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200"
                >
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-800 shrink-0" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800">{val}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

// === FILE_START: src/components/AcademicsSection.tsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { BookOpen, Check, Award, Calendar, FileText, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { ClassItem, SubjectItem } from '../types';

interface AcademicsSectionProps {
  classes: ClassItem[];
  subjects: SubjectItem[];
  onApplyForClass: (className: string) => void;
}

export const AcademicsSection: React.FC<AcademicsSectionProps> = ({
  classes,
  subjects,
  onApplyForClass,
}) => {
  const [selectedClassId, setSelectedClassId] = useState<string>(classes[3]?.id || classes[0]?.id || '');

  const selectedClass = classes.find(c => c.id === selectedClassId) || classes[0];
  const classSubjects = subjects.filter(s => s.class_name === selectedClass?.name);

  return (
    <section id="academics" className="py-20 bg-neutral-50 border-b border-neutral-200">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-6 mb-12">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 tracking-wider uppercase mb-2">
              Academic Curricula & Programs
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-neutral-950 font-display tracking-tight text-balance">
              Rigorous Education from Early Years to College
            </h2>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600 max-w-md text-pretty">
            Structured in full alignment with the Board of Intermediate and Secondary Education (BISE) Dera Ismail Khan
            and National Single Curriculum standards.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Academic Program Segmented Selector (Functional interactive tabs) */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-4 mb-8 no-scrollbar">
          {classes.map(cls => (
            <button
              key={cls.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedClassId(cls.id)}
              className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer ${
                cls.id === selectedClass?.id
                  ? 'bg-emerald-950 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-white text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 border border-neutral-200'
              }`}
            >
              {cls.name}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Selected Program Showcase */}
        {selectedClass && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 p-6 sm:p-10 shadow-sm grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">
            <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-6">
              <div>
                <div className="text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                  {selectedClass.grade_level}
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display">
                  {selectedClass.name}
                </h3>
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                  <span>Stream: <strong className="text-neutral-800">{selectedClass.stream}</strong></span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span>Head Teacher: <strong className="text-neutral-800">{selectedClass.class_teacher}</strong></span>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                  <span>Capacity: <strong className="text-neutral-800">{selectedClass.capacity} Students</strong></span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Curriculum Overview */}
              <div className="pt-4 border-t border-neutral-100">
                <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-500 mb-3">
                  Core Subjects & Period Allocations
                </h4>
                {classSubjects.length > 0 ? (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                    {classSubjects.map(sub => (
                      <div
                        key={sub.id}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-150 text-xs"
                      >
                        <div>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900 block">{sub.name}</span>
                          <span className="text-neutral-500 text-[11px]">Instructor: {sub.teacher_name}</span>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-800 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-emerald-200">
                          {sub.weekly_periods} Periods/wk
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 italic">
                    Comprehensive multi-disciplinary curriculum with specialized periods for English, Urdu, Mathematics, General Science, and Nazra Quran.
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Assessment and Pedagogy */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-4 border-t border-neutral-100">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <Award className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h5 className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-900 mb-0.5">Board Examination Focus</h5>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600">
                      Regular diagnostic model tests aligned with BISE Dera Ismail Khan question patterns.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-900 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <Calendar className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h5 className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-900 mb-0.5">Continuous Monitoring</h5>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600">
                      Monthly progress cards and scheduled Parent-Teacher Conferences (PTM) each term.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Right Summary & Application Card */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-neutral-900 text-white rounded-xl p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-6">
              <div>
                <span className="text-xs text-emerald-400 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1">
                  Tuition & Enrollment
                </span>
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold font-mono text-white">
                  PKR {selectedClass.monthly_tuition_fee.toLocaleString()}
                  <span className="text-xs font-sans text-neutral-400 font-normal"> / month</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
                  Merit-based scholarships available for students scoring 85%+ in previous school examinations.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2.5 text-xs text-neutral-300 pt-4 border-t border-neutral-800">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>Free laboratory practical sessions included</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>Library reading and computer access</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                  <span>Digital student & parent portal record access</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="pt-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => onApplyForClass(selectedClass.name)}
                  className="w-full py-3 px-4 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-600 rounded-lg shadow-md transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <span>Apply for {selectedClass.name}</span>
                  <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/AcademicsSection.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/AdminAiAssistant.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Sparkles,
  Send,
  Database,
  ShieldCheck,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  RefreshCw,
  MessageSquare,
  AlertCircle,
  X,
  ArrowRight,
  BookOpen,
  UserCheck,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { AiResponseRenderer } from './ui/AiResponseRenderer';

interface Message {
  id: string;
  sender: 'user' | 'assistant';
  text: string;
  timestamp: string;
  isError?: boolean;
  toolUsed?: string;
  data?: any;
  disambiguationRequired?: boolean;
  candidates?: any[];
  requiresConfirmation?: boolean;
  pendingAction?: {
    actionType: string;
    title: string;
    description: string;
    payload: any;
    recipientCount?: number;
    previewText?: string;
  };
  auditLogId?: string;
}

export const AdminAiAssistant: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([
    {
      id: 'welcome',
      sender: 'assistant',
      text: `### Shawan School & College Admin AI Assistant\n\nQuery live school collections with role-authorized verification.\n\n* **Faculty Records:** Request complete teacher profiles, qualifications, or assigned classes (e.g. *"Give me the complete record of teacher Muhammad Ali"*)\n* **Student Dossiers:** Inspect student profiles, guardian contacts, attendance rates, or fee ledgers (e.g. *"Show me Ahmad Khan's complete student profile"*)\n* **Operational Audits:** Filter low attendance, check today's absentees, or list unpaid fee vouchers\n* **Controlled Actions:** Draft fee reminders or prepare parent SMS alerts with mandatory administrator confirmation\n\nSelect a suggested inquiry below or enter a question:`,
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
      toolUsed: 'systemInit',
    },
  ]);

  const [inputPrompt, setInputPrompt] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'chat' | 'audit' | 'categories'>('chat');
  const [auditLogs, setAuditLogs] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [auditLoading, setAuditLoading] = useState(false);
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<{ title: string; prompts: string[] }[]>([]);
  const [activeEntity, setActiveEntity] = useState<{
    type: 'student' | 'teacher';
    id: string;
    name: string;
    className?: string;
    admissionNo?: string;
    rollNo?: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Sensitive Action Confirmation Modal State
  const [pendingConfirmAction, setPendingConfirmAction] = useState<{
    actionType: string;
    title: string;
    description: string;
    payload: any;
    recipientCount?: number;
    previewText?: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [actionExecuting, setActionExecuting] = useState(false);
  const [actionSuccessMsg, setActionSuccessMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [actionErrorMsg, setActionErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const scrollToBottom = () => {
    const prefersReduced =
      typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
      window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: prefersReduced ? 'auto' : 'smooth' });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    scrollToBottom();
  }, [messages, loading]);

  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .getAdminAiSamplePrompts()
      .then(res => {
        if (res?.categories) {
          setCategories(res.categories);
        }
      })
      .catch(err => console.warn('Failed to load sample prompts', err));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pendingConfirmAction) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && !actionExecuting) {
        setPendingConfirmAction(null);
        setActionErrorMsg(null);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [pendingConfirmAction, actionExecuting]);

  const loadAuditLogs = async () => {
    setAuditLoading(true);
    try {
      const logs = await api.getAdminAiAuditLogs();
      setAuditLogs(logs || []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load AI audit logs', err);
    } finally {
      setAuditLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSend = async (
    queryText?: string,
    selectedCandidateId?: string,
    overrideEntity?: {
      type: 'student' | 'teacher';
      id: string;
      name: string;
      className?: string;
      admissionNo?: string;
      rollNo?: string;
    }
  ) => {
    const textToSend = (queryText || inputPrompt).trim();
    if (!textToSend || loading) return;

    setActionSuccessMsg(null);
    setActionErrorMsg(null);

    const userMsg: Message = {
      id: `user-${Date.now()}`,
      sender: 'user',
      text: textToSend,
      timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    };

    setMessages(prev => [...prev, userMsg]);
    setInputPrompt('');
    setLoading(true);

    const targetEntity = overrideEntity || activeEntity || undefined;

    try {
      const result = await api.queryAdminAi(textToSend, {
        selectedId: selectedCandidateId,
        activeEntity: targetEntity,
      });

      if (result.activeEntity) {
        setActiveEntity(result.activeEntity);
      }

      const botMsg: Message = {
        id: `bot-${Date.now()}`,
        sender: 'assistant',
        text: result.text,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
        toolUsed: result.toolUsed,
        data: result.data,
        disambiguationRequired: result.disambiguationRequired,
        candidates: result.candidates,
        requiresConfirmation: result.requiresConfirmation,
        pendingAction: result.pendingAction,
        auditLogId: result.auditLogId,
      };

      setMessages(prev => [...prev, botMsg]);

      if (result.requiresConfirmation && result.pendingAction) {
        setPendingConfirmAction(result.pendingAction);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errorMsg: Message = {
        id: `err-${Date.now()}`,
        sender: 'assistant',
        isError: true,
        text: `Error querying school database: ${err.message || 'Unable to retrieve records. Please verify your administrator session and try again.'}`,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
      };
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, errorMsg]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmAction = async () => {
    if (!pendingConfirmAction) return;
    setActionExecuting(true);
    setActionErrorMsg(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.executeAdminAiAction(
        pendingConfirmAction.actionType,
        pendingConfirmAction.payload,
        true
      );

      setActionSuccessMsg(res.message);
      setPendingConfirmAction(null);

      const confirmBotMsg: Message = {
        id: `confirmed-${Date.now()}`,
        sender: 'assistant',
        text: `### Action Executed & Verified\n\n${res.message}\n\n* **Dispatched Recipients:** ${res.dispatchedCount || 0}\n* **Audit Reference:** \`${res.auditLogId || 'Logged'}\``,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
        toolUsed: 'executeConfirmedAction',
      };
      setMessages(prev => [...prev, confirmBotMsg]);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setActionErrorMsg(err.message || 'Action execution failed. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setActionExecuting(false);
    }
  };

  const quickInquiries = [
    { label: 'New Admitted Students', query: 'Show new admitted students.' },
    { label: 'Admissions This Month', query: 'Show recent admissions this month.' },
    { label: 'Teacher Details & Classes', query: 'Show teacher details and assigned classes.' },
    { label: 'Student Attendance & Fees', query: 'Show student attendance and fee status.' },
    { label: 'Parent Details', query: 'Show parent details for this student.' },
    { label: 'Student Complete Record', query: 'Give me the complete record of this student.' },
    { label: 'Attendance < 75%', query: 'Show students with attendance below 75%.' },
    { label: 'Muhammad Ali Record', query: 'Give me the complete record of teacher Muhammad Ali.' },
    { label: 'Ahmad Khan Profile', query: "Show me Ahmad Khan's complete student profile." },
    { label: 'Teachers for Class 9', query: 'Show teachers assigned to Class 9.' },
    { label: 'Class 8 Enrollment', query: 'How many students are enrolled in Class 8?' },
    { label: "Today's Absent", query: "Show today's absent students." },
    { label: 'Unpaid Fees List', query: 'Show students with unpaid fees.' },
    { label: 'Draft Fee Reminder', query: 'Draft fee reminder for students with unpaid fees.' },
    { label: 'Absent SMS Alert', query: 'Send SMS alert to absent students.' },
  ];

  const adminDisplayName = user?.name || 'Administrator';

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col min-h-[560px] lg:h-[calc(100vh-120px)] bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-xs">
      {/* Top Header */}
      <div className="px-4 sm:px-6 py-4 bg-white border-b border-neutral-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-900 text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900 truncate">
              Admin AI Assistant
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>Authorized Administrative Access</span>
              <span aria-hidden="true">&middot;</span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-800 font-medium">
                <Database className="w-3 h-3" />
                Live School Database
              </span>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Navigation Tabs */}
        <div
          role="tablist"
          aria-label="Admin AI Assistant sections"
          className="flex items-center bg-neutral-100 p-1 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 text-xs overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar shrink-0"
        >
          <button
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={activeTab === 'chat'}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('chat')}
            className={`min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
              activeTab === 'chat'
                ? 'bg-white text-emerald-950 shadow-2xs border border-neutral-200/80'
                : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
            }`}
          >
            <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>AI Chat</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={activeTab === 'categories'}
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('categories')}
            className={`min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
              activeTab === 'categories'
                ? 'bg-white text-emerald-950 shadow-2xs border border-neutral-200/80'
                : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
            }`}
          >
            <BookOpen className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Query Topics</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={activeTab === 'audit'}
            onClick={() => {
              setActiveTab('audit');
              loadAuditLogs();
            }}
            className={`min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer ${
              activeTab === 'audit'
                ? 'bg-white text-emerald-950 shadow-2xs border border-neutral-200/80'
                : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
            }`}
          >
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Audit Logs</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Action Status Feedback Banner */}
      {actionSuccessMsg && (
        <div
          role="status"
          className="px-4 sm:px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-50 border-b border-emerald-200 text-emerald-900 text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
        >
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-700 shrink-0" />
            <span>{actionSuccessMsg}</span>
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActionSuccessMsg(null)}
            aria-label="Dismiss notification"
            className="p-1 text-emerald-700 hover:text-emerald-950 rounded cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Body: Chat */}
      {activeTab === 'chat' && (
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 bg-neutral-50/60">
          {/* Quick Suggestion Bar */}
          <div className="px-3 sm:px-6 py-2.5 bg-white border-b border-neutral-200 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-500 shrink-0">
              Suggested:
            </span>
            {quickInquiries.map((item, idx) => (
              <button
                key={idx}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSend(item.query)}
                disabled={loading}
                className="min-h-[32px] shrink-0 text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-neutral-100 hover:bg-emerald-50 text-neutral-700 hover:text-emerald-900 border border-neutral-200 hover:border-emerald-300 transition-colors font-medium whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {item.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {/* Messages Scroll Area */}
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 sm:px-6 py-5 space-y-5">
            {messages.map(msg => {
              const isUser = msg.sender === 'user';
              const showVerificationBanner = !isUser && msg.id !== 'welcome';

              return (
                <div
                  key={msg.id}
                  className={`flex flex-col ${isUser ? 'items-end' : 'items-start'}`}
                >
                  {/* Metadata Line */}
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 mb-1.5 px-1 text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-700">
                      {isUser ? adminDisplayName : 'Admin AI Assistant'}
                    </span>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">&middot;</span>
                    <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{msg.timestamp}</span>
                    {msg.toolUsed && msg.toolUsed !== 'systemInit' && (
                      <>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">&middot;</span>
                        <span className="font-mono text-emerald-800">
                          {msg.toolUsed}
                        </span>
                      </>
                    )}
                    {msg.auditLogId && (
                      <>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">&middot;</span>
                        <span className="text-neutral-500">Audit recorded</span>
                      </>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Message Card */}
                  <div
                    className={`${
                      isUser
                        ? 'max-w-[90%] sm:max-w-[75%] bg-emerald-900 text-white rounded-2xl rounded-tr-xs px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-relaxed shadow-2xs'
                        : 'w-full sm:max-w-[92%] bg-white text-neutral-800 border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-xs p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-2xs'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {isUser ? (
                      <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{msg.text}</p>
                    ) : (
                      <AiResponseRenderer
                        text={msg.text}
                        isError={msg.isError}
                        showStatusBanner={showVerificationBanner}
                        intentLabel={msg.toolUsed !== 'systemInit' ? msg.toolUsed : undefined}
                      />
                    )}

                    {/* Candidate Disambiguation Cards */}
                    {msg.disambiguationRequired && msg.candidates && msg.candidates.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="mt-4 p-3.5 sm:p-4 bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl space-y-3">
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 text-amber-950 text-xs">
                          <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                          <div>
                            <p className="font-bold">Multiple Matching Records Found</p>
                            <p className="text-amber-800 mt-0.5">
                              Select the exact student or faculty record below to load verified details:
                            </p>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                          {msg.candidates.map((cand: any, idx: number) => {
                            const isStudentCandidate = Boolean(cand.class_name || cand.admission_no);
                            return (
                              <button
                                key={idx}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() =>
                                  handleSend(cand.name, cand.databaseId || cand.id, {
                                    type: isStudentCandidate ? 'student' : 'teacher',
                                    id: cand.databaseId || cand.id,
                                    name: cand.name,
                                    className: cand.class_name,
                                    rollNo: cand.roll_no,
                                    admissionNo: cand.admission_no,
                                  })
                                }
                                className="min-h-[52px] p-3 bg-white hover:bg-emerald-50/60 rounded-xl text-left border border-amber-200/90 hover:border-emerald-400 transition-colors flex items-center justify-between gap-3 group cursor-pointer shadow-2xs"
                              >
                                <div className="min-w-0">
                                  <div className="font-bold text-neutral-900 text-xs truncate">
                                    {cand.name}
                                  </div>
                                  <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-600 mt-0.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 font-mono tabular-nums">
                                    {isStudentCandidate ? (
                                      <>
                                        <span>{cand.class_name}</span>
                                        {cand.roll_no && (
                                          <>
                                            <span aria-hidden="true">&middot;</span>
                                            <span>Roll #{cand.roll_no}</span>
                                          </>
                                        )}
                                        {cand.admission_no && (
                                          <>
                                            <span aria-hidden="true">&middot;</span>
                                            <span>{cand.admission_no}</span>
                                          </>
                                        )}
                                      </>
                                    ) : (
                                      <span>{cand.subject || cand.designation || 'Faculty'}</span>
                                    )}
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                                <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 group-hover:text-emerald-700 transition-colors shrink-0" />
                              </button>
                            );
                          })}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Action Confirmation Prompt Card */}
                    {msg.requiresConfirmation && msg.pendingAction && (
                      <div className="mt-4 p-3.5 sm:p-4 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <div className="space-y-1">
                          <div className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-950 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700 shrink-0" />
                            <span>{msg.pendingAction.title}</span>
                          </div>
                          <p className="text-xs text-amber-900">
                            {msg.pendingAction.description}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setActionErrorMsg(null);
                            setPendingConfirmAction(msg.pendingAction!);
                          }}
                          className="min-h-[38px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-800 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-colors shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          Review &amp; Confirm
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}

            {loading && (
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5" role="status" aria-live="polite">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-800 animate-spin" />
                </div>
                <div className="bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl rounded-tl-xs px-4 py-3 text-xs text-neutral-700 shadow-2xs">
                  Querying verified school database records...
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
          </div>

          {/* Active Conversational Follow-up Context Bar */}
          {activeEntity && (
            <div className="px-3 sm:px-6 py-2.5 bg-emerald-50/80 border-t border-emerald-200 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 text-emerald-950">
                <UserCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-800 shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">
                  Follow-up context ({activeEntity.type === 'student' ? 'Student' : 'Faculty'}):{' '}
                  <strong className="font-bold">{activeEntity.name}</strong>
                  {activeEntity.className ? ` · ${activeEntity.className}` : ''}
                  {activeEntity.admissionNo ? ` · ${activeEntity.admissionNo}` : ''}
                </span>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveEntity(null)}
                className="min-h-[32px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
              >
                Clear Context
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Input Bar */}
          <div className="p-3 sm:p-4 bg-white border-t border-neutral-200">
            <form
              onSubmit={e => {
                e.preventDefault();
                handleSend();
              }}
              className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-2"
            >
              <label htmlFor="admin-ai-query-input" className="sr-only">
                Ask Admin AI Assistant a question about school records
              </label>
              <input
                id="admin-ai-query-input"
                type="text"
                value={inputPrompt}
                onChange={e => setInputPrompt(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ask about teachers, student attendance, fees, admissions, or draft reminders..."
                disabled={loading}
                className="flex-1 min-h-[44px] bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 focus:bg-white focus:border-emerald-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-neutral-900 placeholder-neutral-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700/20 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || !inputPrompt.trim()}
                className="min-h-[44px] px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-white font-bold text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                <span>Ask Assistant</span>
                <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </form>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-2 px-1">
              <span>Grounded exclusively in verified database records</span>
              <span>Role-restricted &middot; Administrative audit trail active</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Query Topics & Examples Tab */}
      {activeTab === 'categories' && (
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-6 space-y-6 bg-neutral-50/40">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-neutral-200 pb-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900">Curated Inquiry Categories</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-0.5">
                Select any sample question below to run a live database lookup.
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('chat')}
              className="min-h-[38px] self-start sm:self-auto px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white hover:bg-neutral-100 border border-neutral-200 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
            >
              Back to Chat
            </button>
          </div>

          {categories.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl text-xs text-neutral-500">
              No curated inquiry categories available right now. Use the AI Chat tab to enter any question directly.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              {categories.map((cat, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={idx}
                  className="bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3"
                >
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-900 tracking-wide">
                    {cat.title}
                  </h4>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {cat.prompts.map((p, pIdx) => (
                      <button
                        key={pIdx}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setActiveTab('chat');
                          handleSend(p);
                        }}
                        className="w-full min-h-[42px] text-left p-3 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 hover:bg-emerald-50/70 border border-neutral-200 hover:border-emerald-300 text-xs text-neutral-800 hover:text-emerald-950 transition-colors flex items-center justify-between gap-2 group cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <span className="flex-1">{p}</span>
                        <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400 group-hover:text-emerald-800 transition-colors shrink-0" />
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Audit Logs Tab */}
      {activeTab === 'audit' && (
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-6 space-y-4 bg-neutral-50/40">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-neutral-200 pb-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-800 shrink-0" />
                <span>AI Assistant Governance &amp; Audit Trail</span>
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-0.5">
                Every database lookup and confirmed administrative action executed through the assistant is logged here.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={loadAuditLogs}
                disabled={auditLoading}
                className="min-h-[38px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white hover:bg-neutral-100 border border-neutral-200 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${auditLoading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
                <span>Refresh</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab('chat')}
                className="min-h-[38px] px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition-colors whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
              >
                Back to Chat
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {auditLoading ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl text-neutral-600 text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
              <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-emerald-800" />
              <span>Loading audit records...</span>
            </div>
          ) : auditLogs.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl text-neutral-500 text-xs">
              No AI assistant queries have been recorded yet. Run a query in the AI Chat tab to populate the audit trail.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left text-xs text-neutral-700 border-collapse">
                  <thead className="bg-neutral-50 text-neutral-600 border-b border-neutral-200">
                    <tr>
                      <th scope="col" className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">Action</th>
                      <th scope="col" className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">Admin User</th>
                      <th scope="col" className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">Target</th>
                      <th scope="col" className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">Details</th>
                      <th scope="col" className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">Timestamp</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-100">
                    {auditLogs.map((log, idx) => (
                      <tr key={idx} className="hover:bg-neutral-50/80 transition-colors">
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono font-semibold text-emerald-900 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {log.action}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-900 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {log.user_name} <span className="text-neutral-500">({log.user_role})</span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-600 whitespace-nowrap">{log.target_type}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-neutral-700 max-w-md truncate" title={log.details}>
                          {log.details}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-mono tabular-nums text-neutral-500 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {log.timestamp ? new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleString() : '—'}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Sensitive Action Confirmation Modal */}
      {pendingConfirmAction && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-neutral-950/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          onClick={() => {
            if (!actionExecuting) {
              setPendingConfirmAction(null);
              setActionErrorMsg(null);
            }
          }}
        >
          <div
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="ai-confirm-action-title"
            onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
            className="bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl max-w-md w-full p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4"
          >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-700 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 id="ai-confirm-action-title" className="text-base font-bold text-neutral-900">
                    Administrator Confirmation Required
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-0.5">
                    Review the details below before dispatching this action.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setPendingConfirmAction(null);
                  setActionErrorMsg(null);
                }}
                disabled={actionExecuting}
                aria-label="Close confirmation modal"
                className="p-1.5 text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 rounded-lg hover:bg-neutral-100 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {actionErrorMsg && (
              <div
                role="alert"
                className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-800 text-xs flex items-start gap-2"
              >
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>{actionErrorMsg}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-2 text-xs text-neutral-700">
              <div className="font-bold text-neutral-900 text-sm">{pendingConfirmAction.title}</div>
              <p className="text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">{pendingConfirmAction.description}</p>
              {pendingConfirmAction.previewText && (
                <div className="mt-2 p-3 bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-lg text-neutral-800 font-mono text-xs whitespace-pre-wrap">
                  &ldquo;{pendingConfirmAction.previewText}&rdquo;
                </div>
              )}
              {pendingConfirmAction.recipientCount !== undefined && (
                <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-amber-900 pt-1 tabular-nums">
                  Target Recipients: {pendingConfirmAction.recipientCount} parent/guardian contact(s)
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col-reverse sm:flex-row sm:justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-neutral-100">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setPendingConfirmAction(null);
                  setActionErrorMsg(null);
                }}
                disabled={actionExecuting}
                className="min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-neutral-100 hover:bg-neutral-200 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleConfirmAction}
                disabled={actionExecuting}
                className="min-h-[40px] px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {actionExecuting ? (
                  <>
                    <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                    <span>Executing...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    <span>Confirm &amp; Execute</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/AdminAiAssistant.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/AdminDashboard.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Users,
  GraduationCap,
  BookOpen,
  Calendar,
  CreditCard,
  Clock,
  Award,
  Bell,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Settings as SettingsIcon,
  Mail,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Menu,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Edit2,
  CheckCircle2,
  Search,
  Check,
  X,
  FileText,
  DollarSign,
  AlertCircle,
  Save,
  Shield,
  Printer,
  Phone,
  MapPin,
  User as UserIcon,
  UserCheck,
  UserPlus,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  Unlink,
  Eye,
  Download,
  ExternalLink,
  Filter,
  Sparkles,
  Database,
  Server,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { AdminAiAssistant } from './AdminAiAssistant';
import { ConfirmDialog } from './ui/ConfirmDialog';
import {
  Student,
  Teacher,
  ClassItem,
  SubjectItem,
  AdmissionApplication,
  AttendanceRecord,
  FeeRecord,
  TimetableEntry,
  Exam,
  ResultRecord,
  Announcement,
  SchoolEvent,
  GalleryItem,
  ContactMessage,
  SchoolSettings,
} from '../types';

interface AdminDashboardProps {
  onBackToWebsite: () => void;
  onRefreshSiteSettings?: () => void;
}

export const AdminDashboard: React.FC<AdminDashboardProps> = ({
  onBackToWebsite,
  onRefreshSiteSettings,
}) => {
  const { user, login } = useAuth();
  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState<string>('dashboard');
  const [loginLoading, setLoginLoading] = useState(false);
  const [loginError, setLoginError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Dashboard Stats State
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<any>({
    totalStudents: 0,
    totalTeachers: 0,
    pendingAdmissions: 0,
    approvedAdmissions: 0,
    todayMarked: 0,
    presentToday: 0,
    attendanceRate: 100,
    totalPendingFee: 0,
    totalCollectedFee: 0,
    upcomingEvents: 0,
    totalAnnouncements: 0,
    unreadMessages: 0,
    recentAnnouncements: [],
    recentAdmissions: [],
    recentFees: [],
  });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Module States
  const [students, setStudents] = useState<Student[]>([]);
  const [teachers, setTeachers] = useState<Teacher[]>([]);
  const [classes, setClasses] = useState<ClassItem[]>([]);

  const [announcements, setAnnouncements] = useState<Announcement[]>([]);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<SchoolEvent[]>([]);
  const [gallery, setGallery] = useState<GalleryItem[]>([]);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ContactMessage[]>([]);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<SchoolSettings | null>(null);

  // Filters, Pagination & Responsive Drawer States
  const [mobileSidebarOpen, setMobileSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [studentSearch, setStudentSearch] = useState('');
  const [studentClassFilter, setStudentClassFilter] = useState('all');
  const [studentStatusFilter, setStudentStatusFilter] = useState('all');
  const [studentPage, setStudentPage] = useState(1);

  const [admissionSearch, setAdmissionSearch] = useState('');
  const [admissionStatusFilter, setAdmissionStatusFilter] = useState('all');
  const [admissionPage, setAdmissionPage] = useState(1);

  const [feeSearch, setFeeSearch] = useState('');
  const [feeStatusFilter, setFeeStatusFilter] = useState('all');
  const [feeClassFilter, setFeeClassFilter] = useState('all');
  const [feePage, setFeePage] = useState(1);

  // Accessible Confirmation Dialog State
  const [confirmDialog, setConfirmDialog] = useState<{
    title: string;
    description: string;
    confirmLabel?: string;
    variant?: 'danger' | 'warning' | 'primary';
    onConfirm: () => Promise<void> | void;
  } | null>(null);
  const [confirmLoading, setConfirmLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mobileSidebarOpen) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        setMobileSidebarOpen(false);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [mobileSidebarOpen]);

  const [selectedStudentForModal, setSelectedStudentForModal] = useState<Student | null>(null);
  const [studentDetailLoading, setStudentDetailLoading] = useState(false);
  const [studentDetailData, setStudentDetailData] = useState<{
    student: Student;
    attendance: AttendanceRecord[];
    fees: FeeRecord[];
    results: ResultRecord[];
    timetable: TimetableEntry[];
  } | null>(null);
  const [studentModalActiveTab, setStudentModalActiveTab] = useState<'overview' | 'attendance' | 'fees' | 'results' | 'timetable'>('overview');
  const [isEditingStudent, setIsEditingStudent] = useState(false);
  const [editStudentForm, setEditStudentForm] = useState<Partial<Student>>({});
  const [showPrintIdCard, setShowPrintIdCard] = useState(false);
  const [showAddStudentModal, setShowAddStudentModal] = useState(false);
  const [showAddTeacherModal, setShowAddTeacherModal] = useState(false);
  const [showAddClassModal, setShowAddClassModal] = useState(false);
  const [showAddNoticeModal, setShowAddNoticeModal] = useState(false);
  const [showAddEventModal, setShowAddEventModal] = useState(false);
  const [showRecordFeeModal, setShowRecordFeeModal] = useState<FeeRecord | null>(null);

  // Parent-Child Guardian Relationship Management States
  const [parentLinksData, setParentLinksData] = useState<{
    parents: Array<{
      id: string;
      name: string;
      email: string;
      phone: string;
      linked_students: Student[];
    }>;
    all_students: Array<{
      id: string;
      admission_no: string;
      full_name: string;
      father_name: string;
      class_name: string;
      section: string;
      roll_no: string;
      parent_user_id?: string;
    }>;
  } | null>(null);
  const [parentLinksLoading, setParentLinksLoading] = useState(false);
  const [parentLinksSearch, setParentLinksSearch] = useState('');
  const [showManualLinkModal, setShowManualLinkModal] = useState(false);
  const [manualLinkParentId, setManualLinkParentId] = useState('');
  const [manualLinkStudentId, setManualLinkStudentId] = useState('');
  const [manualLinkLoading, setManualLinkLoading] = useState(false);

  // Timetable State
  const [timetableClassFilter, setTimetableClassFilter] = useState<string>('All');
  const [timetableDayFilter, setTimetableDayFilter] = useState<string>('All');
  const [timetableViewMode, setTimetableViewMode] = useState<'cards' | 'grid'>('cards');
  const [timetableSearch, setTimetableSearch] = useState<string>('');
  const [editingTimetableId, setEditingTimetableId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showAddTimetableModal, setShowAddTimetableModal] = useState(false);
  const [newTimetable, setNewTimetable] = useState<Partial<TimetableEntry>>({
    day: 'Monday',
    start_time: '08:30 AM',
    end_time: '09:15 AM',
    room: 'Room 101',
    section: 'A',
  });

  // Exams & Results State
  const [examsActiveTab, setExamsActiveTab] = useState<'exams' | 'results'>('exams');
  const [editingExamId, setEditingExamId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showAddExamModal, setShowAddExamModal] = useState(false);
  const [newExam, setNewExam] = useState<Partial<Exam>>({
    term: 'First Term',
    academic_year: '2026-2027',
  });
  const [editingResultId, setEditingResultId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showRecordResultModal, setShowRecordResultModal] = useState(false);
  const [selectedDmcResult, setSelectedDmcResult] = useState<ResultRecord | null>(null);
  const [showDmcModal, setShowDmcModal] = useState(false);
  const [resultSearch, setResultSearch] = useState<string>('');
  const [newResultForm, setNewResultForm] = useState<{
    exam_id: string;
    student_id: string;
    subjects: Array<{ subject_name: string; total_marks: number; obtained_marks: number; grade: string }>;
    remarks: string;
    published: boolean;
  }>({
    exam_id: '',
    student_id: '',
