      {/* Official DMC Detailed Marks Certificate Printable Modal */}
      {showDmcModal && selectedDmcResult && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl max-w-2xl w-full p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl border border-neutral-200 relative my-8">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setShowDmcModal(false);
                setSelectedDmcResult(null);
              }}
              className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 p-1 cursor-pointer print:hidden"
            >
              <X className="w-6 h-6" />
            </button>

            {/* Official Certificate Container */}
            <div className="border-4 border-double border-emerald-900/40 p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl bg-white space-y-6">
              {/* Header */}
              <div className="text-center border-b-2 border-emerald-900/30 pb-4">
                <div className="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-emerald-900 text-amber-300 font-bold text-xl mb-2 shadow-xs">
                  SSC
                </div>
                <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-emerald-950 font-display uppercase tracking-wide">
                  Shawan School & College Paharpur
                </h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 font-medium">
                  Affiliated with BISE Dera Ismail Khan · Khyber Pakhtunkhwa
                </p>
                <div className="mt-2 inline-block px-4 py-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-900 text-white font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Detailed Marks Certificate (DMC)
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Student Bio */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs bg-neutral-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">Student Name:</span>
                  <div className="font-bold text-neutral-900 text-sm">{selectedDmcResult.student_name}</div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">Class & Section:</span>
                  <div className="font-bold text-neutral-900">{selectedDmcResult.class_name} {selectedDmcResult.section ? `(${selectedDmcResult.section})` : ''}</div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">Exam Term:</span>
                  <div className="font-semibold text-emerald-900">{selectedDmcResult.exam_title}</div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-neutral-500">Certificate Status:</span>
                  <div className="font-semibold text-emerald-800">
                    {selectedDmcResult.published !== false ? 'Official / Published' : 'Provisional Draft'}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Subjects Table */}
              <div className="border border-neutral-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
                  <thead className="bg-emerald-900 text-white font-bold">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="py-2.5 px-3">#</th>
                      <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Subject</th>
                      <th className="py-2.5 px-3 text-center">Total Marks</th>
                      <th className="py-2.5 px-3 text-center">Marks Obtained</th>
                      <th className="py-2.5 px-3 text-center">Grade</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-neutral-200">
                    {(selectedDmcResult.subjects || []).map((sub, i) => (
                      <tr key={i} className="hover:bg-neutral-50/70">
                        <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono text-neutral-400">{i + 1}</td>
                        <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-semibold text-neutral-900">{sub.subject_name}</td>
                        <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono text-center">{sub.total_marks}</td>
                        <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-mono font-bold text-center text-emerald-950">{sub.obtained_marks}</td>
                        <td className="py-2.5 px-3 font-bold text-center">
                          <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] ${
                            sub.grade.includes('A') ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900' : 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-900'
                          }`}>
                            {sub.grade}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                    <tr className="bg-neutral-100/80 font-bold">
                      <td colSpan={2} className="py-3 px-3 text-neutral-900">Aggregate Total:</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 font-mono text-center">{selectedDmcResult.total_marks}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 font-mono text-center text-emerald-950 text-sm">{selectedDmcResult.obtained_marks}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 text-center text-emerald-900">{selectedDmcResult.overall_grade}</td>
                    </tr>
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>

              {/* Remarks and Grading Summary */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-xs pt-2">
                <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50/70 border border-emerald-200/70 space-y-1">
                  <div className="font-bold text-emerald-950">Overall Percentage: <span className="font-mono text-base">{selectedDmcResult.percentage}%</span></div>
                  <div className="text-neutral-600">Overall Grade: <strong className="text-emerald-900">{selectedDmcResult.overall_grade}</strong></div>
                  <div className="text-neutral-600">Faculty Remarks: <em>"{selectedDmcResult.remarks}"</em></div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col justify-end text-center pt-6 space-y-6">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-[10px] text-neutral-500 pt-8 border-t border-dashed border-neutral-300">
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-neutral-800">Controller of Examinations</div>
                      <div>Shawan School & College</div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-neutral-800">Principal / Headmaster</div>
                      <div>Official Institutional Seal</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Bottom Actions */}
            <div className="pt-5 flex items-center justify-end gap-3 print:hidden">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setShowDmcModal(false);
                  setSelectedDmcResult(null);
                }}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-600 bg-neutral-100 hover:bg-neutral-200 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
              >
                Close
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => window.print()}
                className="px-5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 rounded-xl cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
              >
                <Printer className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Print Official DMC</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Lightbox Photo Preview Modal */}
      {selectedGalleryPhoto && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="relative max-w-4xl w-full bg-neutral-900 rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border border-neutral-800 flex flex-col">
            <button
              onClick={() => setSelectedGalleryPhoto(null)}
              className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-10 bg-black/60 hover:bg-black text-white p-2 rounded-full cursor-pointer transition-colors"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>

            <div className="h-[60vh] bg-black flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
              <img
                src={selectedGalleryPhoto.image_url}
                alt={selectedGalleryPhoto.title}
                className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 bg-neutral-900 text-white flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-emerald-900/60 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-700 mb-1">
                  {selectedGalleryPhoto.category}
                </span>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold font-display">{selectedGalleryPhoto.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-400 mt-1 max-w-xl">{selectedGalleryPhoto.caption}</p>
              </div>

              <div className="text-right text-xs text-neutral-400 shrink-0">
                <div>Date: {selectedGalleryPhoto.date}</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] font-mono mt-0.5 text-neutral-500">ID: {selectedGalleryPhoto.id}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Accessible Administrative Confirmation Dialog */}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={Boolean(confirmDialog)}
        title={confirmDialog?.title || 'Confirm Action'}
        description={confirmDialog?.description || ''}
        confirmLabel={confirmDialog?.confirmLabel || 'Confirm'}
        variant={confirmDialog?.variant || 'danger'}
        loading={confirmLoading}
        onCancel={() => {
          if (!confirmLoading) setConfirmDialog(null);
        }}
        onConfirm={async () => {
          if (!confirmDialog) return;
          setConfirmLoading(true);
          try {
            await confirmDialog.onConfirm();
            setConfirmDialog(null);
          } finally {
            setConfirmLoading(false);
          }
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/AdminDashboard.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/AdmissionsSection.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Send, Search, CheckCircle2, Clock, AlertCircle, Copy, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { ClassItem, AdmissionApplication } from '../types';

interface AdmissionsSectionProps {
  classes: ClassItem[];
  preselectedClass?: string;
}

export const AdmissionsSection: React.FC<AdmissionsSectionProps> = ({
  classes,
  preselectedClass,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'apply' | 'track'>('apply');

  // Form State
  const [studentName, setStudentName] = useState('');
  const [fatherName, setFatherName] = useState('');
  const [dob, setDob] = useState('');
  const [gender, setGender] = useState<'Male' | 'Female' | 'Other'>('Male');
  const [classApplying, setClassApplying] = useState(preselectedClass || classes[0]?.name || 'Grade 9 - Matric');
  const [previousSchool, setPreviousSchool] = useState('');
  const [parentPhone, setParentPhone] = useState('');
  const [parentEmail, setParentEmail] = useState('');
  const [address, setAddress] = useState('');
  const [bFormProvided, setBFormProvided] = useState(true);
  const [slcProvided, setSlcProvided] = useState(true);

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [submitError, setSubmitError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submittedApp, setSubmittedApp] = useState<AdmissionApplication | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  // Tracking State
  const [trackRef, setTrackRef] = useState('');
  const [trackingLoading, setTrackingLoading] = useState(false);
  const [trackedApp, setTrackedApp] = useState<AdmissionApplication | null>(null);
  const [trackError, setTrackError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (preselectedClass) {
      setClassApplying(preselectedClass);
    }
  }, [preselectedClass]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitError(null);

    if (!studentName.trim() || !fatherName.trim() || !parentPhone.trim() || !classApplying) {
      setSubmitError('Please fill out all required fields marked with (*).');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const documents = [];
      if (bFormProvided) {
        documents.push({
          title: 'Student B-Form / Birth Certificate',
          filename: 'b_form_attached.pdf',
          verified: false,
        });
      }
      if (slcProvided) {
        documents.push({
          title: 'School Leaving Certificate / DMC',
          filename: 'slc_dmc_attached.pdf',
          verified: false,
        });
      }

      const res = await api.applyAdmission({
        student_name: studentName.trim(),
        father_name: fatherName.trim(),
        dob,
        gender,
        class_applying: classApplying,
        previous_school: previousSchool.trim(),
        parent_phone: parentPhone.trim(),
        parent_email: parentEmail.trim(),
        address: address.trim(),
        documents,
      });

      setSubmittedApp(res.application);
      const prefersReducedMotion =
        typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
      if (!prefersReducedMotion) {
        confetti({
          particleCount: 70,
          spread: 55,
          origin: { y: 0.6 },
        });
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setSubmitError(err.message || 'Failed to submit application. Please verify your details and try again.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleTrack = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!trackRef.trim()) return;

    setTrackingLoading(true);
    setTrackError(null);
    setTrackedApp(null);

    try {
      const app = await api.trackAdmission(trackRef.trim());
      setTrackedApp(app);
    } catch {
      setTrackError('No application found with this reference number. Please verify the code and try again.');
    } finally {
      setTrackingLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const copyRefToClipboard = (text: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  return (
    <section id="admissions" className="py-16 sm:py-20 bg-white border-b border-neutral-200">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="max-w-3xl mb-10 sm:mb-12">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 tracking-wider uppercase mb-2">
            Admissions &amp; Student Registration
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-neutral-950 font-display tracking-tight text-balance mb-4">
            Join Shawan School and College
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed text-pretty">
            Online admission applications are open for the 2026–2027 academic session. Submit an application online or
            track the real-time status of an existing reference number.
          </p>

          {/* Accessible Segmented Tabs (320px-safe) */}
          <div
            role="tablist"
            aria-label="Admission options"
            className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 sm:inline-grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-1 p-1 bg-neutral-100 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 w-full sm:w-auto"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={activeTab === 'apply'}
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('apply')}
              className={`min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                activeTab === 'apply'
                  ? 'bg-white text-neutral-900 shadow-2xs border border-neutral-200/80'
                  : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
              }`}
            >
              1. Online Admission Form
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={activeTab === 'track'}
              onClick={() => setActiveTab('track')}
              className={`min-h-[40px] px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                activeTab === 'track'
                  ? 'bg-white text-neutral-900 shadow-2xs border border-neutral-200/80'
                  : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
              }`}
            >
              2. Track Application Status
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'apply' ? (
          <div>
            {submittedApp ? (
              <div
                role="status"
                className="max-w-2xl mx-auto bg-emerald-50/70 border border-emerald-200 rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-10 text-center"
              >
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-emerald-800 text-white rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 shadow-xs">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-950 font-display mb-2">
                  Application Submitted Successfully
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-emerald-900 mb-6 max-w-md mx-auto leading-relaxed">
                  Your admission application for <strong>{submittedApp.student_name}</strong> into{' '}
                  <strong>{submittedApp.class_applying}</strong> has been recorded by the Admissions Office.
                </p>

                <div className="bg-white p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 mb-6 inline-block w-full max-w-sm">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-500 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1">
                    Application Reference Number
                  </span>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-lg sm:text-2xl font-mono font-bold text-neutral-900 tracking-wider tabular-nums">
                      {submittedApp.application_no}
                    </span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => copyRefToClipboard(submittedApp.application_no)}
                      aria-label="Copy application reference number"
                      className="min-h-[36px] min-w-[36px] p-1.5 text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-900 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 hover:bg-neutral-50 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center"
                      title="Copy Reference"
                    >
                      {copied ? <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-700" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="text-xs text-neutral-700 space-y-1.5 mb-8 max-w-md mx-auto">
                  <p>Save this reference number to check your admission review progress online.</p>
                  <p>
                    Our admissions office will contact the guardian at{' '}
                    <strong className="font-mono">{submittedApp.parent_phone}</strong>.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-center gap-3">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setTrackRef(submittedApp.application_no);
                      setActiveTab('track');
                    }}
                    className="min-h-[42px] px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Track Status Now
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setSubmittedApp(null);
                      setStudentName('');
                      setFatherName('');
                      setParentPhone('');
                    }}
                    className="min-h-[42px] px-5 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 bg-white border border-neutral-300 hover:bg-neutral-50 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Submit Another Application
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form
                onSubmit={handleSubmit}
                className="bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-10 shadow-2xs space-y-8"
              >
                {submitError && (
                  <div
                    role="alert"
                    className="p-4 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-rose-800 flex items-start gap-2.5"
                  >
                    <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-rose-600 mt-0.5" />
                    <span>{submitError}</span>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Section 1: Desired Class */}
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-900 mb-4 pb-2 border-b border-neutral-200">
                    1. Desired Academic Level &amp; Class
                  </h3>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adm-class" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Class Applying For *
                      </label>
                      <select
                        id="adm-class"
                        value={classApplying}
                        onChange={e => setClassApplying(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                        required
                      >
                        {classes.map(c => (
                          <option key={c.id} value={c.name}>
                            {c.name} ({c.grade_level})
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div className="sm:col-span-1 lg:col-span-2">
                      <label htmlFor="adm-prev-school" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Previous School / Institution
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adm-prev-school"
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="e.g. Govt High School / Private Academy"
                        value={previousSchool}
                        onChange={e => setPreviousSchool(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Section 2: Student Details */}
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-900 mb-4 pb-2 border-b border-neutral-200">
                    2. Student Information
                  </h3>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adm-student-name" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Student Full Name *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adm-student-name"
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="As on B-Form / Birth Cert"
                        value={studentName}
                        onChange={e => setStudentName(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                        required
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adm-father-name" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Father / Guardian Name *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adm-father-name"
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Father's complete name"
                        value={fatherName}
                        onChange={e => setFatherName(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                        required
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adm-dob" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Date of Birth
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adm-dob"
                        type="date"
                        value={dob}
                        onChange={e => setDob(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adm-gender" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Gender
                      </label>
                      <select
                        id="adm-gender"
                        value={gender}
                        onChange={e => setGender(e.target.value as 'Male' | 'Female' | 'Other')}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                      >
                        <option value="Male">Male</option>
                        <option value="Female">Female</option>
                        <option value="Other">Other</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Section 3: Parent & Contact Details */}
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-900 mb-4 pb-2 border-b border-neutral-200">
                    3. Parent / Guardian Contact Details
                  </h3>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adm-phone" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Parent Mobile Phone *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adm-phone"
                        type="tel"
                        placeholder="+92 300 0000000"
                        value={parentPhone}
                        onChange={e => setParentPhone(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                        required
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adm-email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Email Address (Optional)
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adm-email"
                        type="email"
                        placeholder="parent@example.com"
                        value={parentEmail}
                        onChange={e => setParentEmail(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adm-address" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                        Residential Address
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adm-address"
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="e.g. Rangpur Road, Paharpur, DI Khan"
                        value={address}
                        onChange={e => setAddress(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Section 4: Document Check & Declaration */}
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-neutral-900 mb-3 pb-2 border-b border-neutral-200">
                    4. Required Documents Checklist
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-neutral-500 mb-3">
                    Original documents are verified in person at the school office upon interview call:
                  </p>
                  <div className="space-y-2.5 text-xs text-neutral-700">
                    <label className="flex items-start gap-2.5 cursor-pointer">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={bFormProvided}
                        onChange={e => setBFormProvided(e.target.checked)}
                        className="mt-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded text-emerald-700 focus:ring-emerald-600"
                      />
                      <span>NADRA B-Form or Union Council Official Birth Certificate copy</span>
                    </label>
                    <label className="flex items-start gap-2.5 cursor-pointer">
                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={slcProvided}
                        onChange={e => setSlcProvided(e.target.checked)}
                        className="mt-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded text-emerald-700 focus:ring-emerald-600"
                      />
                      <span>Previous School Leaving Certificate (SLC) / Matric DMC (for College applicants)</span>
                    </label>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Submission CTA */}
                <div className="pt-4 border-t border-neutral-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
                  <span className="text-xs text-neutral-500">
                    No processing fee is charged for online pre-registration.
                  </span>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={submitting}
                    className="min-h-[44px] px-6 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-50 rounded-xl shadow-xs transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span>{submitting ? 'Submitting Application...' : 'Submit Application'}</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Track Application Tab */
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            <form onSubmit={handleTrack} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5 mb-8">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <label htmlFor="track-ref-input" className="sr-only">
                  Application Reference Number
                </label>
                <input
                  id="track-ref-input"
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Enter reference number (e.g. ADM-2026-7841)"
                  value={trackRef}
                  onChange={e => setTrackRef(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full text-xs sm:text-sm py-3 px-4 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700 font-mono"
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={trackingLoading}
                className="min-h-[44px] px-6 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-50 rounded-xl cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shrink-0"
              >
                <Search className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                <span>{trackingLoading ? 'Searching...' : 'Track Status'}</span>
              </button>
            </form>

            {trackError && (
              <div
                role="alert"
                className="p-4 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-rose-800 mb-6 flex items-start gap-2.5"
              >
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>{trackError}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {trackedApp && (
              <div className="bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-8 shadow-2xs space-y-6">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-4 border-b border-neutral-100">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                      Application Tracking Record
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-lg font-mono font-bold text-neutral-900 tabular-nums">
                      {trackedApp.application_no}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-500 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-0.5">
                      Review Status
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg ${
                        trackedApp.status === 'Approved'
                          ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200'
                          : trackedApp.status === 'Under Review'
                          ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200'
                          : trackedApp.status === 'Rejected'
                          ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-800 border border-rose-200'
                          : 'bg-neutral-100 text-neutral-800 border border-neutral-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {trackedApp.status === 'Approved' && <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      {trackedApp.status === 'Under Review' && <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      {trackedApp.status}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-neutral-500 block">Candidate Name</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">{trackedApp.student_name}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-neutral-500 block">Father Name</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">{trackedApp.father_name}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-neutral-500 block">Class Applied</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">{trackedApp.class_applying}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-neutral-500 block">Submission Date</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900 font-mono">
                      {new Date(trackedApp.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-neutral-500 block">Parent Contact</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900 font-mono">{trackedApp.parent_phone}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-neutral-500 block">Previous School</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-neutral-900">{trackedApp.previous_school || 'Not specified'}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-neutral-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 text-xs">
                  <span className="font-bold text-neutral-900 block mb-1">
                    Administrative Remarks &amp; Next Steps:
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-neutral-700 leading-relaxed">
                    {trackedApp.remarks ||
                      'Your application is under evaluation by the admissions committee. Please bring original B-Form and previous report card to the school office for document verification.'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/AdmissionsSection.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/ContactLocationSection.tsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { MapPin, Phone, Mail, Clock, Send, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { SchoolSettings } from '../types';

interface ContactLocationSectionProps {
  settings: SchoolSettings;
}

export const ContactLocationSection: React.FC<ContactLocationSectionProps> = ({ settings }) => {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [subject, setSubject] = useState('Admission Inquiry');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim() || !message.trim()) {
      setError('Please enter your name and inquiry message.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await api.sendContactMessage({
        name: name.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
        phone: phone.trim(),
        subject,
        message: message.trim(),
      });
      setSuccess(true);
      setName('');
      setEmail('');
      setPhone('');
      setMessage('');
      setTimeout(() => setSuccess(false), 6000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Failed to send message. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <section id="contact" className="py-16 sm:py-20 bg-white border-b border-neutral-200">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="max-w-3xl mb-10 sm:mb-12">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 tracking-wider uppercase mb-2">
            Campus Location &amp; Inquiries
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-neutral-950 font-display tracking-tight text-balance mb-4">
            Visit Our Campus or Get in Touch
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed text-pretty">
            Located on Rangpur Road in Paharpur, Dera Ismail Khan. Our administrative office is open six days a week
            during school hours to assist parents, prospective students, and visitors.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 lg:gap-10">
          {/* Left Column: Contact Form */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-6 bg-neutral-50 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 p-5 sm:p-8">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display mb-1.5">
              Send an Official Inquiry
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mb-6">
              Inquiries are answered directly by our administrative staff within 24 working hours.
            </p>

            {success && (
              <div
                role="status"
                className="p-4 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-emerald-900 mb-6 flex items-start gap-2.5"
              >
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>Thank you. Your inquiry has been received by the school administration office.</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {error && (
              <div
                role="alert"
                className="p-4 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-rose-800 mb-6 flex items-start gap-2.5"
              >
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span>{error}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="contact-name" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                    Your Name *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="contact-name"
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Full name"
                    value={name}
                    onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="contact-phone" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                    Phone Number
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="contact-phone"
                    type="tel"
                    placeholder="+92 300 0000000"
                    value={phone}
                    onChange={e => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="contact-email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                    Email Address
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="contact-email"
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="email@example.com"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="contact-subject" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                    Inquiry Subject
                  </label>
                  <select
                    id="contact-subject"
                    value={subject}
                    onChange={e => setSubject(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  >
                    <option value="Admission Inquiry">Admission Inquiry</option>
                    <option value="Fee Structure & Concession">Fee Structure &amp; Concession</option>
                    <option value="Verification of Records">Verification of Records</option>
                    <option value="Employment & Faculty Careers">Employment &amp; Faculty Careers</option>
                    <option value="General Question">General Question</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="contact-message" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                  Message *
                </label>
                <textarea
                  id="contact-message"
                  rows={4}
                  placeholder="How can our administration assist you?"
                  value={message}
                  onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 px-3 bg-white border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-50 rounded-xl shadow-xs transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span>{loading ? 'Sending Message...' : 'Send Inquiry'}</span>
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>

          {/* Right Column: Verified Map & Official Contact Cards */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-5 flex flex-col justify-between">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200/70 text-emerald-900 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                    <MapPin className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-500 uppercase tracking-wider block">
                      Campus Address &amp; Plus Code
                    </span>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900 mt-0.5">
                      {settings.location}
                    </h4>
                    <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1">
                      Plus Code: <strong className="font-mono text-emerald-900">{settings.google_maps_code}</strong>
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-900 text-xs font-bold mb-1">
                    <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Office Telephone</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-neutral-900 font-mono font-semibold">
                    {settings.phone || '+92 300 0000000'}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
                    Monday – Saturday during school hours
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-900 text-xs font-bold mb-1">
                    <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span>Official Email</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-neutral-900 font-medium break-all">
                    {settings.email || 'info@shawanschool.edu.pk'}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 break-all">
                    Admissions: {settings.admission_email || 'admissions@shawanschool.edu.pk'}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-900 text-xs font-bold mb-1">
                  <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                  <span>Official Campus Timings</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-neutral-700 space-y-1 mt-1">
                  <p><strong>Summer:</strong> {settings.school_timings_summer}</p>
                  <p><strong>Winter:</strong> {settings.school_timings_winter}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Google Maps Embed Frame with Tailwind border-0 */}
            <div className="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-100 shadow-2xs relative">
              <iframe
                title="Shawan School and College Google Maps Location"
                src="https://maps.google.com/maps?q=4X48%2BMRH%2C%20Rangpur%20Road%2C%20Paharpur%2C%20Pakistan&t=&z=15&ie=UTF8&iwloc=&output=embed"
                className="w-full h-56 border-0"
                loading="lazy"
                referrerPolicy="no-referrer-when-downgrade"
              />
              <div className="p-3 bg-white border-t border-neutral-200 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
                <span className="text-neutral-600 font-medium truncate">
                  4X48+MRH, Rangpur Road, Paharpur
                </span>
                <a
                  href="https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=4X48%2BMRH%2C+Rangpur+Road%2C+Paharpur%2C+Pakistan"
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="min-h-[34px] px-3 py-1.5 bg-neutral-900 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-neutral-800 transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 font-semibold"
                >
                  <span>Directions</span>
                  <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

          <div className="p-6 bg-white rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 shadow-2xs">

              </li>
              <li>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onNavigate('news-events')}
                  className="hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left"
                >
                  Board Examination Notices
                </button>
              </li>
              <li>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onNavigate('gallery')}
                  className="hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left"
                >
                  Campus Photo Gallery
                </button>
              </li>
            </ul>
          </div>

          {/* Accreditation Notice */}
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-white">
              Recognition
            </h4>
            <div className="text-xs text-neutral-400 space-y-2 leading-relaxed">
              <p>• Board of Intermediate & Secondary Education (BISE) Dera Ismail Khan</p>
              <p>• Private Schools Regulatory Authority (PSRA) Khyber Pakhtunkhwa</p>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 pt-1">
                District Dera Ismail Khan, KP, Pakistan
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-8 border-t border-neutral-900 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs text-neutral-500">
          <p>© {new Date().getFullYear()} Shawan School and College Paharpur. All rights reserved.</p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-[11px]">
            <span>Rangpur Road, Paharpur</span>
            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
            <span>DI Khan Education Portal</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/Footer.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/GallerySection.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, ZoomIn } from 'lucide-react';
import { GalleryItem } from '../types';

interface GallerySectionProps {
  gallery: GalleryItem[];
}

export const GallerySection: React.FC<GallerySectionProps> = ({ gallery }) => {
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>('All');
  const [activeImage, setActiveImage] = useState<GalleryItem | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeImage) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setActiveImage(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [activeImage]);

  const filteredGallery =
    selectedCategory === 'All'
      ? gallery
      : gallery.filter(g => g.category === selectedCategory);

  return (
    <section id="gallery" className="py-16 sm:py-20 bg-neutral-50 border-b border-neutral-200">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-end justify-between gap-5 mb-10 sm:mb-12">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 tracking-wider uppercase mb-2">
              Campus Life &amp; Visual Tour
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-neutral-950 font-display tracking-tight text-balance">
              Campus Gallery
            </h2>
          </div>

          {/* Category Filter Tabs */}
          <div
            role="tablist"
            aria-label="Filter gallery by category"
            className="flex items-center gap-1 p-1 bg-white border border-neutral-200 rounded-xl text-xs self-start md:self-auto max-w-full overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar"
          >
            {['All', 'Campus', 'Laboratories', 'Academics', 'Sports'].map(cat => (
              <button
                key={cat}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                aria-selected={selectedCategory === cat}
                onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat)}
                className={`min-h-[36px] px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${
                  selectedCategory === cat
                    ? 'bg-neutral-900 text-white shadow-2xs'
                    : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
                }`}
              >
                {cat}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Gallery Grid */}
        {filteredGallery.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
            {filteredGallery.map(item => (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveImage(item)}
                aria-label={`View photo: ${item.title}`}
                className="group relative text-left rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-white border border-neutral-200 hover:border-neutral-300 shadow-2xs cursor-pointer flex flex-col"
              >
                <div className="relative aspect-4/3 overflow-hidden bg-neutral-100 w-full">
                  <img
                    src={item.image_url}
                    alt={item.title}
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
                    referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  />
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-neutral-950/20 group-hover:bg-neutral-950/40 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100">
                    <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-white/95 text-neutral-900 flex items-center justify-center shadow-md">
                      <ZoomIn className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-4 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between w-full">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-emerald-800 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1">
                      {item.category}
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-900 leading-snug line-clamp-1">
                      {item.title}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-1 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
                      {item.caption}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="p-10 rounded-2xl bg-white border border-neutral-200 text-center text-xs text-neutral-500">
            No photographs published under the <strong>{selectedCategory}</strong> category.
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Accessible Lightbox Modal */}
        {activeImage && (
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-neutral-950/85 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
            onClick={() => setActiveImage(null)}
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              aria-labelledby="gallery-lightbox-title"
              className="bg-neutral-950 rounded-2xl max-w-3xl w-full overflow-hidden border border-neutral-800 relative"
              onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveImage(null)}
                aria-label="Close photo preview"
                className="absolute top-3 right-3 sm:top-4 sm:right-4 z-10 min-h-[40px] min-w-[40px] flex items-center justify-center text-white bg-neutral-950/70 hover:bg-neutral-900 border border-white/15 rounded-full cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>

              <div className="aspect-16/10 bg-neutral-900">
                <img
                  src={activeImage.image_url}
                  alt={activeImage.title}
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                  referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="p-5 sm:p-6 text-white">
                <div className="text-xs text-emerald-400 font-semibold mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  {activeImage.category}
                </div>
                <h3 id="gallery-lightbox-title" className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold font-display mb-1.5">
                  {activeImage.title}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-300 leading-relaxed">{activeImage.caption}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/GallerySection.tsx ===
