// === FILE_END: src/components/HeroSection.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/LoginModal.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Lock,
  Mail,
  AlertCircle,
  Shield,
  GraduationCap,
  UserCheck,
  Users,
  CheckCircle2,
  Calendar,
  Phone,
  Home,
  User,
  BookOpen,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { api, establishAuthenticatedSession } from '../services/api';

interface LoginModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: (role: string) => void;
  initialTab?: 'signin' | 'student-signup' | 'parent-signup';
  initialResetToken?: string | null;
  onClearResetToken?: () => void;
}

export const LoginModal: React.FC<LoginModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSuccess,
  initialTab = 'signin',
  initialResetToken = null,
  onClearResetToken,
}) => {
  const { login, refreshUser } = useAuth();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'signin' | 'student-signup' | 'parent-signup'>(initialTab);
  const [signinMode, setSigninMode] = useState<'login' | 'forgot' | 'confirm-reset'>(
    initialResetToken ? 'confirm-reset' : 'login'
  );
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);

  // Sign In & Password Recovery State
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [forgotEmail, setForgotEmail] = useState('');
  const [resetTokenInput, setResetTokenInput] = useState(initialResetToken || '');
  const [resetNewPassword, setResetNewPassword] = useState('');
  const [resetConfirmPassword, setResetConfirmPassword] = useState('');

  // Student Signup State
  const [stdFullName, setStdFullName] = useState('');
  const [stdEmail, setStdEmail] = useState('');
  const [stdPassword, setStdPassword] = useState('');
  const [stdConfirmPassword, setStdConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [stdDob, setStdDob] = useState('2010-05-15');
  const [stdGender, setStdGender] = useState<'Male' | 'Female'>('Male');
  const [stdFatherName, setStdFatherName] = useState('');
  const [stdPhone, setStdPhone] = useState('');
  const [stdAddress, setStdAddress] = useState('Paharpur, Dera Ismail Khan');
  const [stdClassName, setStdClassName] = useState('Grade 9 - Matric');
  const [stdSection, setStdSection] = useState('A');

  // Parent Signup State
  const [parFullName, setParFullName] = useState('');
  const [parEmail, setParEmail] = useState('');
  const [parPassword, setParPassword] = useState('');
  const [parConfirmPassword, setParConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [parPhone, setParPhone] = useState('');
  const [parAddress, setParAddress] = useState('Paharpur, Dera Ismail Khan');
  const [childAdmNo, setChildAdmNo] = useState('');
  const [childDob, setChildDob] = useState('');

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successNotice, setSuccessNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      if (initialResetToken) {
        setActiveTab('signin');
        setSigninMode('confirm-reset');
        setResetTokenInput(initialResetToken);
      } else {
        setActiveTab(initialTab);
        setSigninMode('login');
      }
      setError(null);
      setSuccessNotice(null);
    }
  }, [isOpen, initialTab, initialResetToken]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && !loading) {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, loading, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Handle Sign In Submit
  const handleSignIn = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) {
      setError('Please enter both your registered email address and password.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const user = await login(email.trim(), password);
      onSuccess(user.role);
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Sign in failed. Please verify your email and password.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Handle Student Registration Submit
  const handleStudentSignup = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!stdFullName || !stdEmail || !stdPassword || !stdFatherName || !stdClassName) {
      setError('Please complete all required student fields marked with (*).');
      return;
    }

    if (stdPassword !== stdConfirmPassword) {
      setError('Passwords do not match.');
      return;
    }

    if (stdPassword.length < 6) {
      setError('Password must be at least 6 characters long.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.registerStudent({
        full_name: stdFullName.trim(),
        email: stdEmail.trim().toLowerCase(),
        password: stdPassword,
        confirm_password: stdConfirmPassword,
        dob: stdDob,
        gender: stdGender,
        father_name: stdFatherName.trim(),
        phone: stdPhone.trim(),
        address: stdAddress.trim(),
        class_name: stdClassName,
        section: stdSection,
      });

      await establishAuthenticatedSession(res.token);
      await refreshUser();
      setSuccessNotice('Student account created. Opening your Student Portal...');
      setTimeout(() => {
        onSuccess('student');
        onClose();
      }, 700);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Student registration failed. Please check your details.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Handle Parent Registration Submit
  const handleParentSignup = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!parFullName || !parEmail || !parPassword) {
      setError('Guardian name, email address, and password are required.');
      return;
    }

    if (parPassword !== parConfirmPassword) {
      setError('Passwords do not match.');
      return;
    }

    if (parPassword.length < 6) {
      setError('Password must be at least 6 characters long.');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.registerParent({
        full_name: parFullName.trim(),
        email: parEmail.trim().toLowerCase(),
        password: parPassword,
        confirm_password: parConfirmPassword,
        phone: parPhone.trim(),
        address: parAddress.trim(),
        child_admission_no: childAdmNo.trim() || undefined,
        child_dob: childDob.trim() || undefined,
      });

      await establishAuthenticatedSession(res.token);
      await refreshUser();
      setSuccessNotice('Guardian account registered. Opening Parent Portal...');
      setTimeout(() => {
        onSuccess('parent');
        onClose();
      }, 700);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Parent registration failed. Please verify the entered details.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleForgotPasswordSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const targetEmail = forgotEmail.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!targetEmail) {
      setError('Please enter your registered email address.');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setSuccessNotice(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.requestPasswordReset(targetEmail);
      setSuccessNotice(res.message);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Unable to process password reset request.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmResetSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!resetTokenInput.trim()) {
      setError('Password reset token is missing.');
      return;
    }
    if (resetNewPassword.length < 6) {
      setError('New password must be at least 6 characters long.');
      return;
    }
    if (resetNewPassword !== resetConfirmPassword) {
      setError('New password and confirmation password do not match.');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    setSuccessNotice(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.confirmPasswordReset({
        token: resetTokenInput.trim(),
        new_password: resetNewPassword,
        confirm_password: resetConfirmPassword,
      });
      setResetTokenInput('');
      setResetNewPassword('');
      setResetConfirmPassword('');
      onClearResetToken?.();
      setSigninMode('login');
      setSuccessNotice(res.message);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError(err.message || 'Unable to reset password. The link may have expired.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleQuickLogin = (demoEmail: string, demoPass: string) => {
    setEmail(demoEmail);
    setPassword(demoPass);
    setError(null);
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-neutral-950/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 overflow-y-auto"
      onClick={() => {
        if (!loading) onClose();
      }}
    >
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="portal-auth-modal-title"
        onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
        className="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-xl w-full p-5 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl border border-neutral-200 relative my-auto max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto"
      >
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          disabled={loading}
          aria-label="Close portal authentication dialog"
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 sm:top-5 sm:right-5 min-h-[36px] min-w-[36px] flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        {/* Modal Header */}
        <div className="mb-5 pr-8">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-emerald-800 tracking-wider uppercase mb-1">
            Institutional Portal · Paharpur, D.I. Khan
          </div>
          <h2 id="portal-auth-modal-title" className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display">
            Shawan School &amp; College
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1">
            Authorized portal access for students, parents, faculty members, and administration.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* 320px-safe Segmented Tab Switcher */}
        <div
          role="tablist"
          aria-label="Authentication mode"
          className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 p-1 bg-neutral-100 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 mb-5"
        >
          {[
            { id: 'signin' as const, label: 'Sign In' },
            { id: 'student-signup' as const, label: 'Student Signup' },
            { id: 'parent-signup' as const, label: 'Parent Signup' },
          ].map(tab => {
            const selected = activeTab === tab.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                aria-selected={selected}
                onClick={() => {
                  setActiveTab(tab.id);
                  setError(null);
                  setSuccessNotice(null);
                }}
                className={`min-h-[38px] px-2 py-2 rounded-lg text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-semibold transition-all cursor-pointer text-center leading-tight ${
                  selected
                    ? 'bg-white text-emerald-950 shadow-2xs border border-neutral-200/80'
                    : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
                }`}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Status Alerts */}
        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="p-3.5 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-rose-800 mb-5 flex items-start gap-2.5"
          >
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {successNotice && (
          <div
            role="status"
            className="p-3.5 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl text-xs text-emerald-900 mb-5 flex items-start gap-2.5"
          >
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span>{successNotice}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* 1. SIGN IN TAB */}
        {activeTab === 'signin' && (
          <div>
            {signinMode === 'login' && (
              <>
                <form onSubmit={handleSignIn} className="space-y-4">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="signin-email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                      Registered Email Address
                    </label>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-3 pointer-events-none" />
                      <input
                        id="signin-email"
                        type="email"
                        autoComplete="email"
                        placeholder="name@shawanschool.edu.pk"
                        value={email}
                        onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 pl-9.5 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                        required
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                      <label htmlFor="signin-password" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700">
                        Password
                      </label>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setForgotEmail(email.trim());
                          setError(null);
                          setSuccessNotice(null);
                          setSigninMode('forgot');
                        }}
                        className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-emerald-800 hover:text-emerald-950 hover:underline cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Forgot password?
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    <div className="relative">
                      <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-3 pointer-events-none" />
                      <input
                        id="signin-password"
                        type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                        autoComplete="current-password"
                        placeholder="Enter your password"
                        value={password}
                        onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 pl-9.5 pr-10 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                        required
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setShowPassword(p => !p)}
                        aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                        className="absolute right-2.5 top-2 p-1 text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 rounded cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={loading}
                    className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-50 rounded-xl shadow-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {loading ? 'Signing in to Portal...' : 'Sign In to Portal'}
                  </button>
                </form>

                {/* Quick Institutional Role Accounts (Development Only — Stripped from Production Builds) */}
                {import.meta.env.DEV && (
                  <div className="mt-6 pt-5 border-t border-neutral-200">
                    <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2.5 text-center">
                      Quick Role Selection (Demo Credentials)
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleQuickLogin('admin@shawanschool.edu.pk', 'AdminPassword123!')}
                        className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:border-emerald-300 border border-neutral-200 text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-neutral-900">
                          <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-800 shrink-0" />
                          <span>Admin</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 truncate">Principal Office</div>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleQuickLogin('teacher@shawanschool.edu.pk', 'TeacherPassword123!')}
                        className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:border-emerald-300 border border-neutral-200 text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-neutral-900">
                          <UserCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-800 shrink-0" />
                          <span>Teacher</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 truncate">Faculty Portal</div>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleQuickLogin('student@shawanschool.edu.pk', 'StudentPassword123!')}
                        className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:border-emerald-300 border border-neutral-200 text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-neutral-900">
                          <GraduationCap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-800 shrink-0" />
                          <span>Student</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 truncate">Student Portal</div>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleQuickLogin('parent@shawanschool.edu.pk', 'ParentPassword123!')}
                        className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:border-emerald-300 border border-neutral-200 text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-neutral-900">
                          <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-800 shrink-0" />
                          <span>Parent</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 truncate">Guardian Portal</div>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </>
            )}

            {signinMode === 'forgot' && (
              <form onSubmit={handleForgotPasswordSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">
                  Enter the email address registered with your portal account. If an active account matches, a single-use password reset link valid for 15 minutes will be sent.
                </p>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="forgot-email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">
                    Registered Email Address
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-3 pointer-events-none" />
                    <input
                      id="forgot-email"
                      type="email"
                      autoComplete="email"
                      placeholder="name@shawanschool.edu.pk"
                      value={forgotEmail}
                      onChange={e => setForgotEmail(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full text-xs py-2.5 pl-9.5 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                      required
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-50 rounded-xl shadow-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {loading ? 'Sending Reset Instructions...' : 'Send Password Reset Link'}
                </button>
                <div className="text-center">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setError(null);
                      setSuccessNotice(null);
                      setSigninMode('login');
                    }}
                    className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-600 hover:text-emerald-900 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Back to Sign In
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}

            {signinMode === 'confirm-reset' && (
              <form onSubmit={handleConfirmResetSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed">

                </p>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="reset-new-password" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">

                      type="button"

                      {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="reset-confirm-password" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1.5">

                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-3 pointer-events-none" />

                      id="reset-confirm-password"
                      type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                      autoComplete="new-password"

                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}

                      setError(null);
                      setSuccessNotice(null);
                      setResetTokenInput('');
                      onClearResetToken?.();

                    type="text"

                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

                <label htmlFor="std-father" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">

                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />

                    id="std-father"
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="e.g. Tariq Mehmood"
                    value={stdFatherName}
                    onChange={e => setStdFatherName(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="std-email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                Student Email Address (Portal Login) *
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  id="std-email"
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="bilal.student@shawanschool.edu.pk"

                  onChange={e => setStdEmail(e.target.value)}

                />

                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="std-confirm" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">

                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    id="std-confirm"
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Re-enter password"
                    value={stdConfirmPassword}
                    onChange={e => setStdConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="std-dob" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                  Date of Birth *
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    id="std-dob"
                    type="date"
                    value={stdDob}
                    onChange={e => setStdDob(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="std-gender" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">

                </label>
                <select
                  id="std-gender"

                  <option value="Female">Female</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="std-class" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">

                </label>

                    <option value="Playgroup & Nursery">Playgroup &amp; Nursery</option>
                    <option value="Kindergarten (KG)">Kindergarten (KG)</option>
                    <option value="Grade 1 - Primary">Grade 1 - Primary</option>
                    <option value="Grade 5 - Primary">Grade 5 - Primary</option>
                    <option value="Grade 8 - Middle">Grade 8 - Middle</option>
                    <option value="Grade 9 - Matric">Grade 9 - Matric (Science)</option>
                    <option value="Grade 10 - Matric">Grade 10 - Matric (Science)</option>
                    <option value="F.Sc Pre-Medical (Part-I)">F.Sc Pre-Medical</option>
                    <option value="F.Sc Pre-Engineering (Part-I)">F.Sc Pre-Engineering</option>
                    <option value="ICS (Computer Science Part-I)">ICS Computer Science</option>

                <label htmlFor="std-section" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">

                  id="std-section"
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="A"
                  value={stdSection}
                  onChange={e => setStdSection(e.target.value)}

                  <input
                    id="std-address"
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Paharpur, Dera Ismail Khan"
                    value={stdAddress}
                    onChange={e => setStdAddress(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"

              </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-50 rounded-xl shadow-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer mt-2"

            </button>
          </form>

        {/* 3. PARENT SIGNUP TAB */}
        {activeTab === 'parent-signup' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleParentSignup} className="space-y-3.5">

            </div>

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="par-fullname" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                Parent / Guardian Full Name *
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <User className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  id="par-fullname"
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="e.g. Haji Ghulam Mustafa"
                  value={parFullName}
                  onChange={e => setParFullName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="par-email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                Guardian Email Address *
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                <input
                  id="par-email"
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="guardian@example.com"
                  value={parEmail}
                  onChange={e => setParEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="par-pass" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                  Password *
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    id="par-pass"
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Min 6 characters"
                    value={parPassword}
                    onChange={e => setParPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="par-confirm" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                  Confirm Password *
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    id="par-confirm"
                    type="password"
                    placeholder="Re-enter password"
                    value={parConfirmPassword}
                    onChange={e => setParConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="par-phone" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                  Phone / WhatsApp Contact
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Phone className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    id="par-phone"
                    type="tel"
                    placeholder="+92 300 1234567"
                    value={parPhone}
                    onChange={e => setParPhone(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="par-address" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">

                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Home className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5 pointer-events-none" />
                  <input
                    id="par-address"
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Paharpur, Dera Ismail Khan"
                    value={parAddress}
                    onChange={e => setParAddress(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 pl-9 pr-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Optional Child Custody Verification */}
            <div className="border-t border-neutral-200 pt-3">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-600 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1">
                Optional Immediate Child Link
              </span>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2.5">
                You may also link one or more children after signing in from your Parent Portal dashboard.
              </p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="par-admno" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Child Admission No
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="par-admno"
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="e.g. SSC-2026-001"
                    value={childAdmNo}
                    onChange={e => setChildAdmNo(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 px-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl font-mono focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="par-childdob" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 mb-1">
                    Child Date of Birth
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="par-childdob"
                    type="date"
                    value={childDob}
                    onChange={e => setChildDob(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full text-xs py-2 px-3 bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-300 rounded-xl focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-700"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:opacity-50 rounded-xl shadow-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer mt-2"
            >
              {loading ? 'Registering Guardian Account...' : 'Complete Parent Registration'}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/LoginModal.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/Navbar.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Menu, X, User as UserIcon, Shield, LogOut, GraduationCap } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

interface NavbarProps {
  onOpenLogin: () => void;
  onOpenPortal: () => void;
  onOpenAdmission: () => void;
  currentView: 'public' | 'portal' | 'admin' | 'teacher';
  setCurrentView: (view: 'public' | 'portal' | 'admin' | 'teacher') => void;
}

export const Navbar: React.FC<NavbarProps> = ({
  onOpenLogin,
  onOpenAdmission,
  currentView,
  setCurrentView,
}) => {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mobileMenuOpen) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setMobileMenuOpen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [mobileMenuOpen]);

  const scrollBehavior = (): ScrollBehavior =>
    typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches
      ? 'auto'
      : 'smooth';

  const handleNavClick = (anchorId: string) => {
    if (currentView !== 'public') {
      setCurrentView('public');
      setTimeout(() => {
        const el = document.getElementById(anchorId);
        if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: scrollBehavior() });
      }, 80);
    } else {
      const el = document.getElementById(anchorId);
      if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: scrollBehavior() });
    }
    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const resolveUserTargetView = (): 'admin' | 'teacher' | 'portal' => {
    if (!user) return 'portal';
    if (['super_admin', 'admin'].includes(user.role)) return 'admin';
    if (user.role === 'teacher') return 'teacher';
    return 'portal';
  };

  const resolvePortalLabel = (): string => {
    if (!user) return 'Portal Login';
    if (['super_admin', 'admin'].includes(user.role)) return 'Admin Console';
    if (user.role === 'teacher') return 'Teacher Portal';
    if (user.role === 'parent') return 'Parent Portal';
    return 'Student Portal';
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-neutral-200">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 h-16 sm:h-18 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        {/* Zone 1: Institutional Wordmark */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            setCurrentView('public');
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: scrollBehavior() });
            setMobileMenuOpen(false);
          }}
          className="text-left group cursor-pointer min-w-0 shrink"
        >
          <span className="block text-base sm:text-xl lg:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-emerald-950 font-display transition-colors group-hover:text-emerald-800 truncate">
            Shawan School &amp; College
          </span>
        </button>

        {/* Zone 2: Primary Single-Line Navigation Links */}
        <nav
          aria-label="Primary school navigation"
          className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-5 xl:gap-7 text-xs xl:text-sm font-medium text-neutral-600 shrink-0"
        >
          {[
            { id: 'about', label: 'About' },
            { id: 'academics', label: 'Academics' },
            { id: 'admissions', label: 'Admissions' },
            { id: 'facilities', label: 'Facilities' },
            { id: 'news-events', label: 'Notices & Events' },
            { id: 'contact', label: 'Contact' },
          ].map(item => (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleNavClick(item.id)}
              className="hover:text-emerald-900 transition-colors cursor-pointer py-1.5 whitespace-nowrap"
            >
              {item.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </nav>

        {/* Zone 3: Desktop Primary Actions */}
        <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-2.5 shrink-0">
          {user ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setCurrentView(resolveUserTargetView())}
                className={`min-h-[38px] px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap ${
                  currentView !== 'public'
                    ? 'bg-emerald-900 text-white shadow-xs'
                    : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-950 hover:bg-emerald-100 border border-emerald-200'
                }`}
              >
                {['super_admin', 'admin'].includes(user.role) ? (
                  <Shield className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                ) : (
                  <GraduationCap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                )}
                <span>{resolvePortalLabel()}</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={logout}
                title="Sign Out"
                aria-label="Sign out of account"
                className="min-h-[38px] min-w-[38px] p-2 text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-100 rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center"
              >
                <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenLogin}
                className="min-h-[38px] px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700 hover:text-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-100 rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                <UserIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span>Portal Login</span>
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onOpenAdmission}
                className="min-h-[38px] px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 rounded-lg transition-colors shadow-xs cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                Apply Online
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Mobile & Tablet Controls (<1024px) */}
        <div className="flex lg:hidden items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
          {user ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setCurrentView(currentView === 'public' ? resolveUserTargetView() : 'public');
                setMobileMenuOpen(false);
              }}
              className="min-h-[40px] px-3 py-2 text-emerald-950 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
            >
              {currentView === 'public' ? resolvePortalLabel() : 'Website'}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onOpenLogin}
              className="min-h-[40px] px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-2 text-neutral-700 hover:text-neutral-900 bg-neutral-100 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer"
            >
              Portal
            </button>
          )}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(prev => !prev)}
            className="min-h-[40px] min-w-[40px] p-2 text-neutral-700 hover:text-neutral-900 hover:bg-neutral-100 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer"
            aria-label={mobileMenuOpen ? 'Close navigation menu' : 'Open navigation menu'}
            aria-expanded={mobileMenuOpen}
            aria-controls="mobile-navigation-drawer"
          >
            {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Accessible Mobile & Tablet Drawer */}
      {mobileMenuOpen && (
        <nav
          id="mobile-navigation-drawer"
          aria-label="Mobile navigation"
          className="lg:hidden border-t border-neutral-200 bg-white px-4 pt-3 pb-5 space-y-1 shadow-lg"
        >
          {[
            { id: 'about', label: 'About School & Leadership' },
            { id: 'academics', label: 'Academic Programs & Classes' },
            { id: 'admissions', label: 'Online Admissions & Tracking' },
            { id: 'facilities', label: 'Campus Facilities' },
            { id: 'fees', label: 'Tuition & Fee Schedule' },
            { id: 'news-events', label: 'Notices & School Events' },
            { id: 'gallery', label: 'Campus Gallery' },
            { id: 'contact', label: 'Location & Contact' },
          ].map(item => (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleNavClick(item.id)}
              className="block w-full text-left min-h-[42px] px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-neutral-700 hover:text-emerald-950 hover:bg-neutral-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              {item.label}
            </button>
          ))}

          <div className="pt-3 mt-2 border-t border-neutral-200 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            {user ? (
              <>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setCurrentView(resolveUserTargetView());
                    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
                  }}
                  className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-2.5 px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-center text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Open {resolvePortalLabel()}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    logout();
                    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
                  }}
                  className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-2.5 px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-center text-neutral-700 bg-neutral-100 hover:bg-neutral-200 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Sign Out ({user.name})
                </button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onOpenLogin();
                    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
                  }}
                  className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-2.5 px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-center text-neutral-800 bg-neutral-100 hover:bg-neutral-200 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Portal Login / Register
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    onOpenAdmission();
                    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
                  }}
                  className="min-h-[44px] w-full py-2.5 px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-center text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Apply for Admission
                </button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </nav>
      )}
    </header>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/Navbar.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/NewsEventsSection.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Clock, MapPin, AlertCircle, ChevronRight, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { Announcement, SchoolEvent } from '../types';

interface NewsEventsSectionProps {
  announcements: Announcement[];
  events: SchoolEvent[];
}

export const NewsEventsSection: React.FC<NewsEventsSectionProps> = ({
  announcements,
  events,
}) => {
  const [selectedNotice, setSelectedNotice] = useState<Announcement | null>(null);
  const [annCategory, setAnnCategory] = useState<string>('All');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedNotice) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setSelectedNotice(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [selectedNotice]);

  const filteredAnnouncements =
    annCategory === 'All'
      ? announcements
      : announcements.filter(a => a.category === annCategory);

  return (
    <section id="news-events" className="py-16 sm:py-20 bg-white border-b border-neutral-200">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="max-w-3xl mb-10 sm:mb-12">
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800 tracking-wider uppercase mb-2">
            Campus Bulletin &amp; Calendar
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-neutral-950 font-display tracking-tight text-balance mb-4">
            Official Announcements &amp; Upcoming Events
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed text-pretty">
            Stay updated with academic circulars, board exam schedules, and upcoming collegiate activities in Paharpur.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-10">
          {/* Left Column: Announcements */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-6">
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display">
                Notice Board &amp; Circulars
              </h3>

              {/* 320px-safe Segmented Filter */}
              <div
                role="tablist"
                aria-label="Filter notices by category"
                className="flex items-center gap-1 p-1 bg-neutral-100 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 text-xs overflow-x-auto max-w-full no-scrollbar self-start sm:self-auto"
              >
                {['All', 'Admission', 'Exam', 'Academic'].map(cat => (
                  <button
                    key={cat}
                    type="button"
                    role="tab"
                    aria-selected={annCategory === cat}
                    onClick={() => setAnnCategory(cat)}
                    className={`min-h-[34px] px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${
                      annCategory === cat
                        ? 'bg-white text-neutral-900 shadow-2xs'
                        : 'text-neutral-600 hover:text-neutral-900'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {cat}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {filteredAnnouncements.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {filteredAnnouncements.map(ann => (
                  <button
                    key={ann.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedNotice(ann)}
                    className="w-full text-left p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 hover:border-emerald-800/40 hover:bg-emerald-50/20 transition-colors cursor-pointer group flex items-start justify-between gap-4"
                  >
                    <div className="space-y-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-900">{ann.category}</span>
                        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                        <span className="font-mono">{new Date(ann.date).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                        {ann.priority === 'Urgent' && (
                          <>
                            <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                            <span className="text-rose-700 font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                              <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" /> Urgent
                            </span>
                          </>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900 group-hover:text-emerald-900 transition-colors leading-snug">
                        {ann.title}
                      </h4>

                      <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
                        {ann.content}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-neutral-400 group-hover:text-emerald-900 shrink-0 mt-1 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5" />
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="p-8 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 text-center text-xs text-neutral-500">
                No notices published in the <strong>{annCategory}</strong> category right now.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Right Column: Upcoming Events */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-6">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display">
              Upcoming Events Calendar
            </h3>

            {events.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {events.map(ev => (
                  <article
                    key={ev.id}
                    className="rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 overflow-hidden bg-white shadow-2xs group"
                  >
                    <div className="h-36 overflow-hidden bg-neutral-100 relative">
                      <img
                        src={ev.cover_image}
                        alt={ev.title}
                        className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-103"
                        referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                      />
                      <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 bg-neutral-950/85 backdrop-blur-xs text-white text-[11px] font-mono px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md">
                        {new Date(ev.date).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
                          month: 'short',
                          day: 'numeric',
                          year: 'numeric',
                        })}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-5">
                      <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-neutral-900 mb-2 font-display">
                        {ev.title}
                      </h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 leading-relaxed mb-4">
                        {ev.description}
                      </p>

                      <div className="pt-3 border-t border-neutral-100 flex flex-col gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400 shrink-0" />
                          <span>{ev.time}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-neutral-400 shrink-0" />
                          <span className="truncate">{ev.location}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="p-8 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-50 border border-neutral-200 text-center text-xs text-neutral-500">
                No upcoming campus events scheduled right now.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Accessible Notice Detail Modal */}
        {selectedNotice && (
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-neutral-950/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4"
            onClick={() => setSelectedNotice(null)}
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              aria-labelledby="notice-detail-title"
              onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
              className="bg-white rounded-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-5 sm:p-8 shadow-2xl border border-neutral-200 relative max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto"
            >
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedNotice(null)}
                aria-label="Close notice details"
                className="absolute top-4 right-4 sm:top-5 sm:right-5 min-h-[36px] min-w-[36px] flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-700 hover:bg-neutral-100 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-emerald-800 font-semibold mb-2 pr-8">
                <span>{selectedNotice.category} Notice</span>
                <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                <span className="text-neutral-500 font-mono font-normal">
                  {new Date(selectedNotice.date).toLocaleDateString()}
                </span>
              </div>

              <h3 id="notice-detail-title" className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display mb-4 pr-6">
                {selectedNotice.title}
              </h3>

              <div className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-neutral-700 leading-relaxed space-y-3 mb-6 bg-neutral-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200">
                <p>{selectedNotice.content}</p>
              </div>

              <div className="pt-4 border-t border-neutral-200 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs text-neutral-500">
                <span>
                  Issued by: <strong className="text-neutral-800">{selectedNotice.author}</strong>
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedNotice(null)}
                  className="min-h-[38px] px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800 bg-neutral-100 hover:bg-neutral-200 rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Close Notice
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/NewsEventsSection.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/NoticeBanner.tsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { Megaphone, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { Announcement } from '../types';

interface NoticeBannerProps {
  announcements: Announcement[];
  onViewNotice: (notice: Announcement) => void;
}

export const NoticeBanner: React.FC<NoticeBannerProps> = ({ announcements, onViewNotice }) => {
  const urgentNotice = announcements.find(a => a.priority === 'Urgent') || announcements[0];

  if (!urgentNotice) return null;

  return (
    <div className="bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-100 text-xs py-2 px-4 border-b border-emerald-900/60">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-hidden truncate">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-semibold text-emerald-300 shrink-0">
            <Megaphone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Notice:</span>
          </span>
          <span className="truncate text-emerald-200">{urgentNotice.title}</span>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => onViewNotice(urgentNotice)}
          className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-300 hover:text-white font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        >
          <span>Read Details</span>
          <ArrowRight className="w-3 h-3" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
// === FILE_END: src/components/NoticeBanner.tsx ===

// === FILE_START: src/components/StudentPortal.tsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  User as UserIcon,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  Award,
  CreditCard,
  Bell,
  Printer,
  ChevronLeft,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  FileText,
  UserPlus,
  UserMinus,
  Search,
  Users,
  Check,
  Sparkles,
  Send,
  LogOut,
  ShieldCheck,
  Info,
  MapPin,
  Phone,
  Mail,
  GraduationCap,
  Filter,
  CheckCheck,
  RefreshCw,
  HelpCircle,
  BookOpen,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { AiResponseRenderer } from './ui/AiResponseRenderer';
import { Student, AttendanceRecord, FeeRecord, TimetableEntry, ResultRecord, Announcement } from '../types';

interface StudentPortalProps {
  onBackToWebsite: () => void;
}

export const StudentPortal: React.FC<StudentPortalProps> = ({ onBackToWebsite }) => {
  const { user, studentProfile, refreshUser, logout } = useAuth();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<
    'overview' | 'profile' | 'attendance' | 'timetable' | 'results' | 'fees' | 'notices' | 'notifications' | 'ai'
  >('overview');

  const [student, setStudent] = useState<Student | null>(studentProfile);
  const [linkedStudents, setLinkedStudents] = useState<Student[]>([]);
  const [selectedStudentId, setSelectedStudentId] = useState<string>(user?.linked_id || '');

  // Attendance filters: 'all' | 'month' | 'week' | 'custom'
  const [attendanceFilter, setAttendanceFilter] = useState<'all' | 'month' | 'week' | 'custom'>('all');
  const [customStartDate, setCustomStartDate] = useState<string>('');
  const [customEndDate, setCustomEndDate] = useState<string>('');

  // Timetable day filter
  const [timetableDayFilter, setTimetableDayFilter] = useState<string>('all');

  // Real MongoDB Notifications state
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [notifLoading, setNotifLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Portal AI Assistant state
  const [portalAiMessages, setPortalAiMessages] = useState<
    Array<{
      sender: 'user' | 'assistant';
      text: string;
      disambiguationRequired?: boolean;
      candidates?: any[];
      toolUsed?: string;
    }>
  >([
    {
      sender: 'assistant',
      text:
        user?.role === 'parent'
          ? `### Shawan Parent Portal Assistant\n\nAsk questions about verified academic records for your linked children.\n\n* **Attendance:** *"Show my children attendance"* or *"Which of my children have low attendance?"*\n* **Fees & Dues:** *"Which fees are pending?"*\n* **Exam Results:** *"Show my child's latest results"*\n* **Schedule:** *"Show class timetable"*`
          : `### Shawan Student Academic Assistant\n\nAsk questions regarding your personal academic profile, attendance, fees, and class schedule.\n\n* **Attendance:** *"What is my attendance?"* or *"When was I absent?"*\n* **Examination:** *"What are my latest results?"*\n* **Fees:** *"What is my fee balance?"*\n* **Classes:** *"When is my next class?"* or *"Show my timetable"*\n* **Notices:** *"What announcements are there?"*`,
    },
  ]);
  const [portalAiInput, setPortalAiInput] = useState<string>('');
  const [portalAiLoading, setPortalAiLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Child Linking Modal States
  const [showLinkModal, setShowLinkModal] = useState(false);
  const [fatherName, setFatherName] = useState(user?.name || '');
  const [studentName, setStudentName] = useState('');
  const [className, setClassName] = useState('');
  const [rollNo, setRollNo] = useState('');
  const [linkLoading, setLinkLoading] = useState(false);
  const [linkError, setLinkError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [linkSuccess, setLinkSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);

  type LinkModalStep = 'search' | 'confirm_single' | 'select_multiple';
  const [linkStep, setLinkStep] = useState<LinkModalStep>('search');
  const [foundStudents, setFoundStudents] = useState<
    Array<{
      id: string;
      full_name: string;
      class_name: string;
      section: string;
      roll_no: string;
    }>
  >([]);
  const [selectedCandidateId, setSelectedCandidateId] = useState<string>('');

  // Unlink Child Confirmation Modal States
  const [childToUnlink, setChildToUnlink] = useState<Student | null>(null);
  const [unlinkLoading, setUnlinkLoading] = useState(false);
  const [unlinkError, setUnlinkError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [unlinkSuccess, setUnlinkSuccess] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!showLinkModal && !childToUnlink) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (showLinkModal && !linkLoading) {
          setShowLinkModal(false);
          setLinkError(null);
          setLinkStep('search');
        }
        if (childToUnlink && !unlinkLoading) {
          setChildToUnlink(null);
        }
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [showLinkModal, childToUnlink, linkLoading, unlinkLoading]);

  const [attendanceData, setAttendanceData] = useState<{ summary: any; records: AttendanceRecord[] } | null>(null);
  const [fees, setFees] = useState<FeeRecord[]>([]);
  const [results, setResults] = useState<ResultRecord[]>([]);
  const [timetable, setTimetable] = useState<TimetableEntry[]>([]);
  const [announcements, setAnnouncements] = useState<Announcement[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [portalError, setPortalError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Print DMC and Fee Challan states
  const [printingResult, setPrintingResult] = useState<ResultRecord | null>(null);
  const [printingFee, setPrintingFee] = useState<FeeRecord | null>(null);

  // Load Real MongoDB Notifications
  const loadNotifications = async () => {
    try {
      setNotifLoading(true);
      const [notifs, unread] = await Promise.all([
        api.getNotifications().catch(() => []),
        api.getUnreadNotificationCount().catch(() => ({ unreadCount: 0 })),
      ]);
      setNotifications(Array.isArray(notifs) ? notifs : []);
      setUnreadCount(typeof unread?.unreadCount === 'number' ? unread.unreadCount : 0);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Could not load notifications:', err);
    } finally {
      setNotifLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleMarkNotificationRead = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await api.markNotificationRead(id);
      setNotifications(prev => prev.map(n => (n.id === id ? { ...n, read: true } : n)));
      setUnreadCount(prev => Math.max(0, prev - 1));
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Failed to mark notification read:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleMarkAllNotificationsRead = async () => {
    try {
      await api.markAllNotificationsRead();
      setNotifications(prev => prev.map(n => ({ ...n, read: true })));
      setUnreadCount(0);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Failed to mark all notifications read:', err);
    }
  };

  const loadPortalData = async (targetStudentId: string) => {
    if (!targetStudentId) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setPortalError(null);
    try {
      const details = await api.getStudentById(targetStudentId);
      setStudent(details.student);
      setFees(details.fees || []);
      setResults(details.results || []);
      setTimetable(details.timetable || []);

      // Attendance summary & records
      try {
        const att = await api.getStudentAttendance(targetStudentId);
        setAttendanceData(att);
      } catch (attErr: any) {
        console.warn('Attendance load notice:', attErr);
        setPortalError(attErr?.message || 'Unable to load attendance summary.');
      }

      // Notices
      try {
        const anns = await api.getAnnouncements({ audience: 'Students' });
        setAnnouncements(anns);
      } catch (annErr) {
        console.warn('Notices load notice:', annErr);
      }

      // Notifications
      await loadNotifications();
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn('Student portal details notice:', err);
      setPortalError(err?.message || 'Unable to load student academic records at this time.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const loadParentChildren = async () => {
    if (user?.role === 'parent') {
      setPortalError(null);
      try {
        const myKids = await api.getMyStudents();
        setLinkedStudents(myKids);
        if (myKids.length > 0) {
          const target =
            selectedStudentId && myKids.some(k => k.id === selectedStudentId) ? selectedStudentId : myKids[0].id;
          setSelectedStudentId(target);
          await loadPortalData(target);
        } else {
          setStudent(null);
          setLoading(false);
        }
      } catch (e: any) {
        console.warn('Could not load parent children:', e);
        setPortalError(e?.message || 'Unable to load linked children records.');
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    if (user.role === 'parent') {
      loadParentChildren();
    } else if (user.role === 'student') {
      const targetId = user.linked_id || studentProfile?.id || '';
      if (targetId) {
        setSelectedStudentId(targetId);
        loadPortalData(targetId);
      } else {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    } else {
      // Administrator or faculty inspecting portal
      const targetId = selectedStudentId || 'std_01';
      setSelectedStudentId(targetId);
      loadPortalData(targetId);
    }
  }, [user]);

  const handleSelectChild = (childId: string) => {
    setSelectedStudentId(childId);
    loadPortalData(childId);
  };

  // Find Child for linking
  const handleFindChild = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!fatherName.trim() || !studentName.trim() || !className.trim() || !rollNo.trim()) {
      setLinkError(
        'Please fill in all required verification fields: Father/Guardian Name, Child Name, Class, and Roll Number.'
      );
      return;
    }

    setLinkLoading(true);
    setLinkError(null);
    setLinkSuccess(null);

    try {
      const res = await api.findChildToLink({
        father_name: fatherName.trim(),
        student_name: studentName.trim(),
        class_name: className.trim(),
        roll_no: rollNo.trim(),
      });

      if (!res.matches || res.matches.length === 0) {
        setLinkError('Student could not be verified. Please check the information or contact school administration.');
        return;
      }

      setFoundStudents(res.matches);
      if (res.matches.length === 1) {
        setSelectedCandidateId(res.matches[0].id);
        setLinkStep('confirm_single');
      } else {
        setSelectedCandidateId(res.matches[0].id);
        setLinkStep('select_multiple');
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      setLinkError(
        err.message ||
          'Student could not be verified. Please check the information or contact school administration.'
      );
    } finally {
      setLinkLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Confirm and Link Child
  const handleConfirmLink = async (targetId?: string) => {
    const studentIdToLink = targetId || selectedCandidateId;
    if (!studentIdToLink) {
      setLinkError('Please select a student to link.');
      return;
    }

    setLinkLoading(true);
    setLinkError(null);

    try {
      const res = await api.confirmLinkChild({
        student_id: studentIdToLink,
        father_name: fatherName.trim(),
      });

      setLinkSuccess(`Successfully linked ${res.student.full_name} to your guardian account!`);
      setLinkedStudents(res.linked_students);
      setSelectedStudentId(res.student.id);
      await loadPortalData(res.student.id);
      await refreshUser();

      setTimeout(() => {
        setShowLinkModal(false);
        setLinkStep('search');
        setLinkSuccess(null);
        setStudentName('');
        setClassName('');
        setRollNo('');
        setFoundStudents([]);
        setSelectedCandidateId('');
      }, 1400);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setLinkError(
        err.message ||
          'Student could not be verified. Please check the information or contact school administration.'
      );
    } finally {
      setLinkLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Unlink Child
  const handleUnlinkChild = async () => {
    if (!childToUnlink) return;

    setUnlinkLoading(true);
    setUnlinkError(null);

    try {
      const res = await api.unlinkChild(childToUnlink.id);
      setUnlinkSuccess(res.message || `Successfully unlinked ${childToUnlink.full_name}.`);
      setLinkedStudents(res.linked_students);
      await refreshUser();

      if (res.linked_students.length > 0) {
        const nextId = res.active_child_id || res.linked_students[0].id;
        setSelectedStudentId(nextId);
        await loadPortalData(nextId);
      } else {
        setSelectedStudentId('');
        setStudent(null);
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        setChildToUnlink(null);
        setUnlinkSuccess(null);
      }, 1000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setUnlinkError(err.message || 'Failed to unlink child.');
    } finally {
      setUnlinkLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Send query to Portal AI
  const handleSendPortalAi = async (textToSend?: string) => {
    const queryText = (textToSend || portalAiInput).trim();
    if (!queryText || portalAiLoading) return;

    setPortalAiMessages(prev => [...prev, { sender: 'user', text: queryText }]);
    setPortalAiInput('');
    setPortalAiLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await api.queryPortalAi(queryText);
      setPortalAiMessages(prev => [
        ...prev,
        {
          sender: 'assistant',
          text: res.text,
          disambiguationRequired: res.disambiguationRequired,
          candidates: res.candidates,
          toolUsed: res.toolUsed,
        },
      ]);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setPortalAiMessages(prev => [
        ...prev,
        {
          sender: 'assistant',
          text: err.message || 'An error occurred while querying authorized academic records from the database.',
        },
      ]);
    } finally {
      setPortalAiLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Filter Attendance Records based on attendanceFilter
  const getFilteredAttendance = () => {
    const allRecords = attendanceData?.records || [];
    if (attendanceFilter === 'all') {
      return allRecords;
    }

    const now = new Date();

    if (attendanceFilter === 'week') {
      const sevenDaysAgo = new Date();
      sevenDaysAgo.setDate(now.getDate() - 7);
      const threshold = sevenDaysAgo.toISOString().split('T')[0];
      return allRecords.filter(r => r.date >= threshold);
    }

    if (attendanceFilter === 'month') {
      const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date();
      thirtyDaysAgo.setDate(now.getDate() - 30);
      const threshold = thirtyDaysAgo.toISOString().split('T')[0];
      return allRecords.filter(r => r.date >= threshold);
    }

    if (attendanceFilter === 'custom') {
      return allRecords.filter(r => {
        if (customStartDate && r.date < customStartDate) return false;
        if (customEndDate && r.date > customEndDate) return false;
        return true;
      });
    }

    return allRecords;
  };

  const filteredAttendance = getFilteredAttendance();
  const filteredPresentCount = filteredAttendance.filter(r => r.status === 'Present').length;
  const filteredAbsentCount = filteredAttendance.filter(r => r.status === 'Absent').length;
  const filteredLateCount = filteredAttendance.filter(r => r.status === 'Late').length;
  const filteredLeaveCount = filteredAttendance.filter(r => r.status === 'Leave').length;
  const filteredPercentage =
    filteredAttendance.length > 0
      ? Math.round((filteredPresentCount / filteredAttendance.length) * 100)
      : attendanceData?.summary?.percentage || 100;

  // Filter Timetable by day
  const filteredTimetable =
    timetableDayFilter === 'all'
      ? timetable
      : timetable.filter(t => t.day.toLowerCase() === timetableDayFilter.toLowerCase());

  const handlePrint = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout();
    onBackToWebsite();
  };

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-100 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-neutral-200 shadow-sm p-6 sm:p-8 max-w-md w-full text-center space-y-4">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-700 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-neutral-900 font-display">Authentication Required</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-neutral-600 mt-1">
              Please sign in with an authorized student or guardian account to view academic records.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onBackToWebsite}
            className="w-full py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-emerald-900 hover:bg-emerald-800 rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            ← Back to School Website
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-neutral-100 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
        <div className="text-center space-y-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 border-3 border-emerald-900 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-600">Loading Verified Institutional Academic Records...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Printable DMC / Report Card View
  if (printingResult && student) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-white p-8 max-w-4xl mx-auto print:p-0">
