      Array<
        Student & {
          attendance_percentage: number;
          total_days: number;
          presents: number;
        }
      >
    >(`/teacher/students${query ? `?${query}` : ''}`);
  },

  getTeacherAttendance: (params?: {
    class_name?: string;
    section?: string;
    date?: string;
    filter?: string;
    start_date?: string;
    end_date?: string;
  }) => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams(params as Record<string, string>).toString();
    return request<{
      summary: {
        total: number;
        present: number;
        absent: number;
        late: number;
        leave: number;
        percentage: number;
      };
      records: AttendanceRecord[];
    }>(`/teacher/attendance${query ? `?${query}` : ''}`);
  },

  saveTeacherAttendance: (data: {
    date: string;
    class_name: string;
    section: string;
    records: Array<{
      student_id: string;
      student_name?: string;
      status: 'Present' | 'Absent' | 'Late' | 'Leave';
      remarks?: string;
    }>;
  }) =>
    request<{ success: boolean; message: string; count: number }>('/teacher/attendance', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }),

  getTeacherExams: () => request<Exam[]>('/teacher/exams'),

  getTeacherResults: (params?: { exam_id?: string; class_name?: string; subject?: string }) => {
    const query = new URLSearchParams(params as Record<string, string>).toString();
    return request<{
      summary: {
        totalStudentsTested: number;
        averagePercentage: number;
        highestScore: number;
        lowestScore: number;
        passCount: number;
        failCount: number;
      };
      results: ResultRecord[];
    }>(`/teacher/results${query ? `?${query}` : ''}`);
  },

  saveTeacherResults: (data: {
    exam_id: string;
    exam_title?: string;
    student_id: string;
    subject_name: string;
    total_marks: number;
    obtained_marks: number;
    remarks?: string;
  }) =>
    request<{ success: boolean; message: string; result: ResultRecord }>('/teacher/results', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    }),

  getTeacherTimetable: () =>
    request<{
      today: string;
      timetable: TimetableEntry[];
    }>('/teacher/timetable'),

  queryTeacherAi: (prompt: string) =>
    request<{
      text: string;
      toolUsed?: string;
      disambiguationRequired?: boolean;
      candidates?: Array<{ id: string; name: string; class_name: string; roll_no: string }>;
      requiresConfirmation?: boolean;
      pendingAction?: {
        actionType: string;
        title: string;
        description: string;
        payload: any;
      };
    }>('/teacher/ai/query', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ prompt }),
    }),

  executeTeacherAiAction: (actionType: string, payload: any, confirmed: boolean) =>
    request<{ success: boolean; message: string }>('/teacher/ai/execute-action', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({ actionType, payload, confirmed }),
    }),
};

// === FILE_END: src/services/api.ts ===

export interface User {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
  role: 'super_admin' | 'admin' | 'teacher' | 'parent' | 'student';
  phone?: string;
  avatar?: string;
  linked_id?: string;
  linked_student_ids?: string[];
}

export interface AuditLogEntry {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  user_name: string;
  user_role: string;
  action: string;
  target_type: string;
  target_id?: string;
  details?: string;
  timestamp: string;
}

export interface Student {
  id: string;
  admission_no: string;
  full_name: string;
  father_name: string;
  dob: string;
  gender: 'Male' | 'Female' | 'Other';
  class_name: string;
  section: string;
  contact_no: string;
  address: string;
  emergency_contact: string;
  profile_image?: string;
  admission_date: string;
  academic_status: 'Active' | 'Inactive' | 'Graduated' | 'Suspended';
  roll_no: string;
  parent_user_id?: string;
}

export interface Teacher {
  id: string;

  profile_photo?: string;
  qualification: string;
  subject: string;
  classes: string[];

  email: string;
  joining_date: string;
  experience: string;

  bio: string;
}

export interface AdmissionApplication {

  dob: string;

  previous_school: string;
  parent_phone: string;
  parent_email: string;
  address: string;
  documents: { title: string; filename: string; verified?: boolean }[];

  remarks?: string;

}

}

    receipt_no: string;

  }[];
}
